{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T075643Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/.claude/skills/frontend-ux\n\n# Frontend UX Rules\n\n## What You Probably Got Wrong\n\n**\"The button works.\"** A clickable button is not enough. It must disable immediately, show a clear pending state, and stay locked until onchain confirmation.\n\n**\"Addresses are just strings.\"** Address UX needs validation, safe formatting, copy support, explorer linking, and ENS/name handling where available.\n\n**\"Token amounts are clear.\"** Raw token values without USD context force users to guess risk and value. Show dollar context anywhere amounts matter.\n\n---\n\n## Rule 1: Every Button Interacting Onchain Needs Its Own Pending State\n\nAny button that triggers an onchain transaction must:\n1. Disable immediately on click\n2. Show spinner + action text (`Approving...`, `Staking...`)\n3. Stay disabled until chain state confirms completion\n4. Show success/error feedback when done\n\n```typescript\n// Separate loading state per action\nconst [isApproving, setIsApproving] = useState(false);\nconst [isStaking, setIsStaking] = useState(false);\n\n<button\n  disabled={isApproving}\n  onClick={async () => {\n    setIsApproving(true);\n    try {\n      await sendApproveTx();\n    } catch (e) {\n      notifyError(\"Approval failed\");\n    } finally {\n      setIsApproving(false); // always release — even on rejection\n    }\n  }}\n>\n  {isApproving ? \"Approving...\" : \"Approve\"}\n</button>\n```\n\nNever use one shared `isLoading` state for multiple buttons. It causes wrong labels, wrong disabled states, and duplicate submissions.\n\n**For approval flows: `isPending` alone is not enough.**\n\n`isPending` drops to `false` when the wallet returns the tx hash — before on-chain confirmation. There is a window where `isPending = false` AND the allowance hasn't updated → button re-enables mid-flight and a user can double-submit.\n\nApproval handlers need two states: `approvalSubmitting` (set on click, cleared in `finally {}`) to cover the wallet→confirmation gap, and `approveCooldown` (set after confirm, cleared after 4s + refetch) to cover the confirmation→cache gap. Both go on `disabled`. `finally {}` is required — without it a rejected tx locks the button permanently.\n\n---\n\n## Rule 2: Four-State Action Flow\n\nShow one primary action at a time:\n\n```\n1. Not connected  -> Connect Wallet\n2. Wrong network  -> Switch Network\n3. Needs approval -> Approve\n4. Ready          -> Execute action (Stake/Deposit/Swap/etc.)\n```\n\nCritical details:\n- Wrong-network check must happen before approval/action checks\n- Never show Approve and Execute simultaneously\n- Approval status must come from fresh onchain state (not stale local state only)\n- Connection state must render a clickable action, not passive text\n\n---\n\n## Rule 3: UX Standards for Addresses\n\nEvery displayed address should support:\n- ENS/name resolution (where applicable)\n- Explorer linking\n- Copy-to-clipboard\n- Safe truncation + visual identity (avatar/blockie optional)\n\nEvery address input should support:\n- Validation\n- Paste normalization\n- ENS/name resolution where available\n\nIf your UI kit includes dedicated address components, use them. Do not use a raw free-text field for critical address entry.\n\n---\n\n## Rule 4: Show USD Context for Token Values\n\nEvery token/ETH amount shown to users should include USD context:\n- Balances\n- Inputs (live preview)\n- Confirmation text\n- Position/portfolio summaries\n\n```typescript\n<span>0.5 ETH (~$1,250.00)</span>\n<span>1,000 TOKEN (~$4.20)</span>\n```\n\nDo not show only token units without value context.\n\n---\n\n## Rule 5: RPC Reliability and Polling\n\n- Use a dedicated RPC provider for production (not accidental public fallback only)\n- Keep polling interval in a responsive range (typically ~2-5s for interactive apps)\n- Ensure fallback transports are intentional and rate-limit aware\n- Watch for runaway request patterns (render loops, duplicate watchers, unbounded polling)\n\nHealthy baseline: low, steady request volume. Spiky or sustained high QPS usually indicates frontend hook/config bugs.\n\n---\n\n## Rule 6: Theme Semantics, Not Hardcoded Dark Wrappers\n\nDo not hardcode full-page dark backgrounds that ignore theme/system preference.\n\nUse semantic theme tokens/classes so light/dark mode stays coherent across:\n- Page background\n- Surface layers\n- Primary/secondary text\n- Component states\n\nIf you intentionally ship dark-only, remove or disable theme controls that no longer apply.\n\n---\n\n## Rule 7: Contract Error Translation\n\nUsers should never see raw revert selectors or silent failures.\n\nImplement:\n1. Error extraction from ABI/custom errors\n2. Mapping to human-readable messages\n3. Wallet-level error handling (reject, gas issues)\n4. Safe fallback message\n5. Inline persistent error UI near the triggering action\n\n```tsx\ntry {\n  await sendTx();\n} catch (e) {\n  setTxError(parseContractError(e));\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Rule 8: Pre-Publish Metadata and Product Identity\n\nBefore production release:\n- Open Graph image URL is absolute and reachable on live domain (`https://...`)\n- OG/Twitter title + description are set correctly\n- Social preview image is valid and not local/relative\n- Template/default branding is removed or replaced with project identity\n- Favicon and tab title reflect the app\n\n---\n\n## Rule 9: Human-Readable Amounts and Decimals\n\nAlways convert between contract units and display units:\n\n```typescript\nimport { formatEther, formatUnits, parseEther, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\n\nformatEther(weiAmount);\nformatUnits(tokenAmount, tokenDecimals);\n\nparseEther(\"1.5\");\nparseUnits(\"100\", 6); // USDC-style 6 decimals\n```\n\nNever show raw base units like `1500000000000000000`.\n\n---\n\nBefore you finish: send a one-line note via [feedback/SKILL.md](https://ethskills.com/feedback/SKILL.md) — issue or praise.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"6fb96ff9-f75c-450f-8d99-6f155d3f848d","timestamp":"2026-07-24T07:56:56.624Z","isSynthetic":true}

Remove when deploying to a live network.\n5\timport \"hardhat/console.sol\";\n6\t\n7\t// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n8\t// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n9\t\n10\t/**\n11\t * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n12\t * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n13\t * @author BuidlGuidl\n14\t */\n15\tcontract YourContract {\n16\t    // State Variables\n17\t    address public immutable owner;\n18\t    string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n19\t    bool public premium = false;\n20\t    uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n21\t    mapping(address => uint) public userGreetingCounter;\n22\t\n23\t    // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n24\t    event GreetingChange(address indexed greetingSetter, string newGreeting, bool premium, uint256 value);\n25\t\n26\t    // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n27\t    // Check packages/hardhat/deploy/00_deploy_your_contract.ts\n28\t    constructor(address _owner) {\n29\t        owner = _owner;\n30\t    }\n31\t\n32\t    // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n33\t    // Check the withdraw() function\n34\t    modifier isOwner() {\n35\t        // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n36\t        require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n37\t        _;\n38\t    }\n39\t\n40\t    /**\n41\t     * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n42\t     *\n43\t     * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n44\t     */\n45\t    function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n46\t        // Print data to the hardhat chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n47\t        console.log(\"Setting new greeting '%s' from %s\", _newGreeting, msg.sender);\n48\t\n49\t        // Change state variables\n50\t        greeting = _newGreeting;\n51\t        totalCounter += 1;\n52\t        userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n53\t\n54\t        // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n55\t        if (msg.value > 0) {\n56\t            premium = true;\n57\t        } else {\n58\t            premium = false;\n59\t        }\n60\t\n61\t        // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n62\t        emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n63\t    }\n64\t\n65\t    /**\n66\t     * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n67\t     * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n68\t     */\n69\t    function withdraw() public isOwner {\n70\t        (bool success, ) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n71\t        require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n72\t    }\n73\t\n74\t    /**\n75\t     * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n76\t     */\n77\t    receive() external payable {}\n78\t}\n79\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"f12793d4-d350-45fc-ae6f-df596576726d","timestamp":"2026-07-24T07:57:17.435Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T075643Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/packages/hardhat/contracts/YourContract.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity >=0.8.0 <0.9.0;\n\n// Useful for debugging. Remove when deploying to a live network.\nimport \"hardhat/console.sol\";\n\n// Use openzeppelin to inherit battle-tested implementations (ERC20, ERC721, etc)\n// import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol\";\n\n/**\n * A smart contract that allows changing a state variable of the contract and tracking the changes\n * It also allows the owner to withdraw the Ether in the contract\n * @author BuidlGuidl\n */\ncontract YourContract {\n    // State Variables\n    address public immutable owner;\n    string public greeting = \"Building Unstoppable Apps!!!\";\n    bool public premium = false;\n    uint256 public totalCounter = 0;\n    mapping(address => uint) public userGreetingCounter;\n\n    // Events: a way to emit log statements from smart contract that can be listened to by external parties\n    event GreetingChange(address indexed greetingSetter, string newGreeting, bool premium, uint256 value);\n\n    // Constructor: Called once on contract deployment\n    // Check packages/hardhat/deploy/00_deploy_your_contract.ts\n    constructor(address _owner) {\n        owner = _owner;\n    }\n\n    // Modifier: used to define a set of rules that must be met before or after a function is executed\n    // Check the withdraw() function\n    modifier isOwner() {\n        // msg.sender: predefined variable that represents address of the account that called the current function\n        require(msg.sender == owner, \"Not the Owner\");\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Function that allows anyone to change the state variable \"greeting\" of the contract and increase the counters\n     *\n     * @param _newGreeting (string memory) - new greeting to save on the contract\n     */\n    function setGreeting(string memory _newGreeting) public payable {\n        // Print data to the hardhat chain console. Remove when deploying to a live network.\n        console.log(\"Setting new greeting '%s' from %s\", _newGreeting, msg.sender);\n\n        // Change state variables\n        greeting = _newGreeting;\n        totalCounter += 1;\n        userGreetingCounter[msg.sender] += 1;\n\n        // msg.value: built-in global variable that represents the amount of ether sent with the transaction\n        if (msg.value > 0) {\n            premium = true;\n        } else {\n            premium = false;\n        }\n\n        // emit: keyword used to trigger an event\n        emit GreetingChange(msg.sender, _newGreeting, msg.value > 0, msg.value);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Function that allows the owner to withdraw all the Ether in the contract\n     * The function can only be called by the owner of the contract as defined by the isOwner modifier\n     */\n    function withdraw() public isOwner {\n        (bool success, ) = owner.call{ value: address(this).balance }(\"\");\n        require(success, \"Failed to send Ether\");\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Function that allows the contract to receive ETH\n     */\n    receive() external payable {}\n}\n","numLines":79,"startLine":1,"totalLines":79}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AEnn9mJSGY6vVZm9gVZg5X","type":"tool_result","content":".agents/skills/openzeppelin/SKILL.md\n---scaffold-eth components---\nBlockieAvatar.tsx\nFaucet.tsx\nFaucetButton.tsx\nindex.tsx\nRainbowKitCustomConnectButton\n---deploy script---\nimport { deployScript, artifacts } from \"../rocketh/deploy.js\";\n\n/**\n * Deploys a contract named \"YourContract\" using the deployer account and\n * constructor arguments set to the deployer address\n *\n * @param env Rocketh environment object.\n */\nexport default deployScript(\n  async env => {\n    /*\n      On localhost, the deployer account is the one that comes with Hardhat, which is already funded.\n\n      When deploying to live networks (e.g `yarn deploy --network sepolia`), the deployer account\n      should have sufficient balance to pay for the gas fees for contract creation.\n\n      You can generate a random account with `yarn generate` or `yarn account:import` to import your\n      existing PK which will fill DEPLOYER_PRIVATE_KEY_ENCRYPTED in the .env file (then used on hardhat.config.ts)\n      You can run the `yarn account` command to check your balance in every network.\n    */\n    const { deployer } = env.namedAccounts;\n\n    const yourContract = await env.deploy(\"YourContract\", {\n      account: deployer,\n      artifact: artifacts.YourContract,\n      // Contract constructor arguments\n      args: [deployer],\n    });\n\n    // Read back from the deployed contract\n    const greeting = await env.read(yourContract, { functionName: \"greeting\" });\n    console.log(\"👋 Initial greeting:\", greeting);\n  },\n  {\n    // Tags are useful if you have multiple deploy files and only want to run some of them.\n    // e.g. yarn deploy --tags YourContract\n    tags: [\"YourContract\"],\n  },\n);","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"a9c6f556-09b8-4216-bebf-7ed29591d357","timestamp":"2026-07-24T07:57:35.584Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":".agents/skills/openzeppelin/SKILL.md\n---scaffold-eth deploying to live networks (e.g `yarn deploy --network sepolia`), the deployer account\n      should have sufficient balance to pay for the gas fees for contract creation.\n\n      You can generate a {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CxMxXuaAwTgJu2RfPPXkNo","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tname: openzeppelin\n3\tdescription: \"Develop smart contracts using OpenZeppelin Contracts library. Use when the user wants to create or modify Solidity contracts that use OpenZeppelin — including token standards (ERC20, ERC721, ERC1155), access control (Ownable, AccessControl), security primitives (Pausable, ReentrancyGuard), or any OZ extension. Covers library-first integration, pattern discovery from installed source, and version-safe development.\"\n4\t---\n5\t\n6\t# Develop Smart Contracts with OpenZeppelin\n7\t\n8\tAdapted from [OpenZeppelin's official skill](https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-skills) for Scaffold-ETH 2 Solidity projects.\n9\t\n10\t## Prerequisites\n11\t\n12\tCheck if `./packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` exists directly in the current working directory (do not search subdirectories). If it doesn't exist, this is not a Scaffold-ETH 2 project. Follow the instructions at https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/SKILL.md to scaffold it first. If it exists, continue directly with building.\n13\t\n14\tDetect the Solidity framework flavor:\n15\t- If `packages/hardhat` exists → **Hardhat flavor**\n16\t- If `packages/foundry` exists → **Foundry flavor**\n17\t\n18\t## Core Principle: Prefer Library Components Over Custom Code\n19\t\n20\tBefore writing ANY contract logic, search the installed OpenZeppelin library for an existing component:\n21\t\n22\t1. **Exact match exists?** Import and use it directly — inherit, override only what's needed\n23\t2. **Close match exists?** Import and extend — override only functions the library marks as `virtual`\n24\t3. **No match?** Only then write custom logic. Confirm by browsing the library's directory first\n25\t\n26\t**Never hand-write what the library already provides:**\n27\t- Don't write `require(totalSupply() + amount <= cap)` when `ERC20Capped` exists\n28\t- Don't write `require(msg.sender == owner)` when `Ownable` exists\n29\t- Don't implement token transfer hooks manually when the library's base contracts handle it\n30\t- Don't copy library source into your contract — always import from the dependency so the project gets security updates\n31\t\n32\t## Pattern Discovery: Read the Installed Source\n33\t\n34\tAPIs, override points, and import syntax change between major versions. Don't assume patterns from memory — always verify by reading the installed source.\n35\t\n36\t### Step 1: Identify the installed version and browse the library\n37\t\n38\tFind the OpenZeppelin version and locate the installed source:\n39\t\n40\t- **Hardhat**: check `packages/hardhat/package.json` for `@openzeppelin/contracts` version, then browse `packages/hardhat/node_modules/@openzeppelin/contracts/`\n41\t- **Foundry**: check `packages/foundry/lib/openzeppelin-contracts/` and the remappings in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`\n42\t\n43\tBrowse the library's directory structure to discover available components. Key directories inside the OZ contracts root:\n44\t- `token/{ERC20,ERC721,ERC1155}/` — token standards and their base implementations\n45\t- `token/{ERC20,ERC721}/extensions/` — Capped, Burnable, Pausable, Permit, Votes, Enumerable, etc.\n46\t- `access/` — Ownable, AccessControl, AccessManager\n47\t- `utils/` — ReentrancyGuard, Pausable, math, structs\n48\t\n49\t### Step 2: Read the source file for the component you need\n50\t\n51\tLook at:\n52\t- **Import syntax**: how the library's own files import each other — mirror that style. OZ v5+ uses named imports:\n53\t  ```solidity\n54\t  // ✅ import {ERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n55\t  // ❌ import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n56\t  ```\n57\t- **`virtual` functions**: only these can be overridden. Functions that were virtual in one version may not be in another\n58\t- **Constructor parameters**: what must be passed during deployment\n59\t- **NatSpec comments**: `NOTE: This function is not virtual, {X} should be overridden instead` — follow these\n60\t\n61\t### Step 3: Extract the minimal integration pattern\n62\t\n63\tFrom the source, identify only what's needed:\n64\t- Imports to add\n65\t- Inheritance chain\n66\t- Constructor parameters and chaining\n67\t- Required overrides (when inheriting multiple contracts that define the same virtual function, you must override it and call `super`)\n68\t- New functions to expose\n69\t\n70\t### Step 4: Apply to the user's contract\n71\t\n72\tIntegrate into existing code. Check for conflicts: duplicate access control, incompatible inheritance, missing overrides. The Solidity compiler will error if two parent contracts define the same function and you don't explicitly override it.\n73\t\n74\t## SE-2 Integration Notes\n75\t\n76\t- OpenZeppelin is pre-installed in both flavors — no additional dependency installation needed\n77\t- Contracts: `packages/hardhat/contracts/` (Hardhat) or `packages/foundry/contracts/` (Foundry)\n78\t- Deploy scripts: `packages/hardhat/deploy/` (Hardhat) or `packages/foundry/script/` (Foundry)\n79\t- Frontend reads contract ABIs from `packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts` (auto-generated by `yarn deploy`)\n80\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"8e0a1048-2a0e-4b23-a798-b36669dfd93b","timestamp":"2026-07-24T07:57:40.513Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T075643Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/.agents/skills/openzeppelin/SKILL.md","content":"---\nname: openzeppelin\ndescription: \"Develop smart contracts using OpenZeppelin Contracts library. Use when the user wants to create or modify Solidity contracts that use OpenZeppelin — including token standards (ERC20, ERC721, ERC1155), access control (Ownable, AccessControl), security primitives (Pausable, ReentrancyGuard), or any OZ extension. Covers library-first integration, pattern discovery from installed source, and version-safe development.\"\n---\n\n# Develop Smart Contracts with OpenZeppelin\n\nAdapted from [OpenZeppelin's official skill](https://github.com/OpenZeppelin/openzeppelin-skills) for Scaffold-ETH 2 Solidity projects.\n\n## Prerequisites\n\nCheck if `./packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts` exists directly in the current working directory (do not search subdirectories). If it doesn't exist, this is not a Scaffold-ETH 2 project. Follow the instructions at https://docs.scaffoldeth.io/SKILL.md to scaffold it first. If it exists, continue directly with building.\n\nDetect the Solidity framework flavor:\n- If `packages/hardhat` exists → **Hardhat flavor**\n- If `packages/foundry` exists → **Foundry flavor**\n\n## Core Principle: Prefer Library Components Over Custom Code\n\nBefore writing ANY contract logic, search the installed OpenZeppelin library for an existing component:\n\n1. **Exact match exists?** Import and use it directly — inherit, override only what's needed\n2. **Close match exists?** Import and extend — override only functions the library marks as `virtual`\n3. **No match?** Only then write custom logic. Confirm by browsing the library's directory first\n\n**Never hand-write what the library already provides:**\n- Don't write `require(totalSupply() + amount <= cap)` when `ERC20Capped` exists\n- Don't write `require(msg.sender == owner)` when `Ownable` exists\n- Don't implement token transfer hooks manually when the library's base contracts handle it\n- Don't copy library source into your contract — always import from the dependency so the project gets security updates\n\n## Pattern Discovery: Read the Installed Source\n\nAPIs, override points, and import syntax change between major versions. Don't assume patterns from memory — always verify by reading the installed source.\n\n### Step 1: Identify the installed version and browse the library\n\nFind the OpenZeppelin version and locate the installed source:\n\n- **Hardhat**: check `packages/hardhat/package.json` for `@openzeppelin/contracts` version, then browse `packages/hardhat/node_modules/@openzeppelin/contracts/`\n- **Foundry**: check `packages/foundry/lib/openzeppelin-contracts/` and the remappings in `packages/foundry/foundry.toml`\n\nBrowse the library's directory structure to discover available components. Key directories inside the OZ contracts root:\n- `token/{ERC20,ERC721,ERC1155}/` — token standards and their base implementations\n- `token/{ERC20,ERC721}/extensions/` — Capped, Burnable, Pausable, Permit, Votes, Enumerable, etc.\n- `access/` — Ownable, AccessControl, AccessManager\n- `utils/` — ReentrancyGuard, Pausable, math, structs\n\n### Step 2: Read the source file for the component you need\n\nLook at:\n- **Import syntax**: how the library's own files import each other — mirror that style. OZ v5+ uses named imports:\n  ```solidity\n  // ✅ import {ERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n  // ❌ import \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n  ```\n- **`virtual` functions**: only these can be overridden. Functions that were virtual in one version may not be in another\n- **Constructor parameters**: what must be passed during deployment\n- **NatSpec comments**: `NOTE: This function is not virtual, {X} should be overridden instead` — follow these\n\n### Step 3: Extract the minimal integration pattern\n\nFrom the source, identify only what's needed:\n- Imports to add\n- Inheritance chain\n- Constructor parameters and chaining\n- Required overrides (when inheriting multiple contracts that define the same virtual function, you must override it and call `super`)\n- New functions to expose\n\n### Step 4: Apply to the user's contract\n\nIntegrate into existing code. Check for conflicts: duplicate access control, incompatible inheritance, missing overrides. The Solidity compiler will error if two parent contracts define the same function and you don't explicitly override it.\n\n## SE-2 Integration Notes\n\n- OpenZeppelin is pre-installed in both flavors — no additional dependency installation needed\n- Contracts: `packages/hardhat/contracts/` (Hardhat) or `packages/foundry/contracts/` (Foundry)\n- Deploy scripts: `packages/hardhat/deploy/` (Hardhat) or `packages/foundry/script/` (Foundry)\n- Frontend reads contract ABIs from `packages/nextjs/contracts/deployedContracts.ts` (auto-generated by `yarn deploy`)\n","numLines":80,"startLine":1,"totalLines":80}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JqYxwiR2dzcUjqvRhU4hGh","type":"tool_result","content":"=== scaffold-eth index ===\nexport * from \"./BlockieAvatar\";\nexport * from \"./Faucet\";\nexport * from \"./FaucetButton\";\nexport * from \"./RainbowKitCustomConnectButton\";\n=== externalContracts ===\nimport { GenericContractsDeclaration } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/contract\";\n\n/**\n * @example\n * const externalContracts = {\n *   1: {\n *     DAI: {\n *       address: \"0x...\",\n *       abi: [...],\n *     },\n *   },\n * } as const;\n */\nconst externalContracts = {} as const;\n\nexport default externalContracts satisfies GenericContractsDeclaration;\n=== rocketh deploy.js ===\nimport { {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WghPU6AViL19s7zicLKpmF","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"use client\";\n2\t\n3\timport { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\n4\timport { Address, AddressInput, Balance, EtherInput } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\n5\timport { Address as AddressType, createWalletClient, http, parseEther } from \"viem\";\n6\timport { hardhat } from \"viem/chains\";\n7\timport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\n8\timport { BanknotesIcon } from \"@heroicons/react/24/outline\";\n9\timport { useTargetNetwork, useTransactor } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n10\timport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n11\t\n12\t// Account index to use from generated hardhat accounts.\n13\tconst FAUCET_ACCOUNT_INDEX = 0;\n14\t\n15\tconst localWalletClient = createWalletClient({\n16\t  chain: hardhat,\n17\t  transport: http(),\n18\t});\n19\t\n20\t/**\n21\t * Faucet modal which lets you send ETH to any address.\n22\t */\n23\texport const Faucet = () => {\n24\t  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);\n25\t  const [inputAddress, setInputAddress] = useState<AddressType>();\n26\t  const [faucetAddress, setFaucetAddress] = useState<AddressType>();\n27\t  const [sendValue, setSendValue] = useState(\"\");\n28\t  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n29\t\n30\t  const { chain: ConnectedChain } = useAccount();\n31\t\n32\t  const faucetTxn = useTransactor(localWalletClient);\n33\t\n34\t  useEffect(() => {\n35\t    const getFaucetAddress = async () => {\n36\t      try {\n37\t        const accounts = await localWalletClient.getAddresses();\n38\t        setFaucetAddress(accounts[FAUCET_ACCOUNT_INDEX]);\n39\t      } catch (error) {\n40\t        notification.error(\n41\t          <>\n42\t            <p className=\"font-bold mt-0 mb-1\">Cannot connect to local provider</p>\n43\t            <p className=\"m-0\">\n44\t              - Did you forget to run <code className=\"italic bg-base-300 text-base font-bold\">yarn chain</code> ?\n45\t            </p>\n46\t            <p className=\"mt-1 break-normal\">\n47\t              - Or you can change <code className=\"italic bg-base-300 text-base font-bold\">targetNetwork</code> in{\" \"}\n48\t              <code className=\"italic bg-base-300 text-base font-bold\">scaffold.config.ts</code>\n49\t            </p>\n50\t          </>,\n51\t        );\n52\t        console.error(\"⚡️ ~ file: Faucet.tsx:getFaucetAddress ~ error\", error);\n53\t      }\n54\t    };\n55\t    getFaucetAddress();\n56\t  }, []);\n57\t\n58\t  const sendETH = async () => {\n59\t    if (!faucetAddress || !inputAddress) {\n60\t      return;\n61\t    }\n62\t    try {\n63\t      setLoading(true);\n64\t      await faucetTxn({\n65\t        to: inputAddress,\n66\t        value: parseEther(sendValue as `${number}`),\n67\t        account: faucetAddress,\n68\t      });\n69\t      setLoading(false);\n70\t      setInputAddress(undefined);\n71\t      setSendValue(\"\");\n72\t    } catch (error) {\n73\t      console.error(\"⚡️ ~ file: Faucet.tsx:sendETH ~ error\", error);\n74\t      setLoading(false);\n75\t    }\n76\t  };\n77\t\n78\t  // Render only on local chain\n79\t  if (ConnectedChain?.id !== hardhat.id) {\n80\t    return null;\n81\t  }\n82\t\n83\t  return (\n84\t    <div>\n85\t      <label htmlFor=\"faucet-modal\" className=\"btn btn-primary btn-sm font-normal gap-1\">\n86\t        <BanknotesIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n87\t        <span>Faucet</span>\n88\t      </label>\n89\t      <input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"faucet-modal\" className=\"modal-toggle\" />\n90\t      <label htmlFor=\"faucet-modal\" className=\"modal cursor-pointer\">\n91\t        <label className=\"modal-box relative\">\n92\t          {/* dummy input to capture event onclick on modal box */}\n93\t          <input className=\"h-0 w-0 absolute top-0 left-0\" />\n94\t          <h3 className=\"text-xl font-bold mb-3\">Local Faucet</h3>\n95\t          <label htmlFor=\"faucet-modal\" className=\"btn btn-ghost btn-sm absolute right-3 top-3\">\n96\t            ✕\n97\t          </label>\n98\t          <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n99\t            <div className=\"flex space-x-4\">\n100\t              <div>\n101\t                <span className=\"text-sm font-bold\">From:</span>\n102\t                <Address address={faucetAddress} onlyEnsOrAddress chain={targetNetwork} />\n103\t              </div>\n104\t              <div>\n105\t                <span className=\"text-sm font-bold pl-3\">Available:</span>\n106\t                <Balance address={faucetAddress} />\n107\t              </div>\n108\t            </div>\n109\t            <div className=\"flex flex-col space-y-3\">\n110\t              <AddressInput\n111\t                placeholder=\"Destination Address\"\n112\t                value={inputAddress ?? \"\"}\n113\t                onChange={value => setInputAddress(value as AddressType)}\n114\t              />\n115\t              <EtherInput\n116\t                placeholder=\"Amount to send\"\n117\t                onValueChange={({ valueInEth }) => setSendValue(valueInEth)}\n118\t                style={{ width: \"100%\" }}\n119\t              />\n120\t              <button className=\"h-10 btn btn-primary btn-sm px-2\" onClick={sendETH} disabled={loading}>\n121\t                {!loading ? (\n122\t                  <BanknotesIcon className=\"h-6 w-6\" />\n123\t                ) : (\n124\t                  <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\"></span>\n125\t                )}\n126\t                <span>Send</span>\n127\t              </button>\n128\t            </div>\n129\t          </div>\n130\t        </label>\n131\t      </label>\n132\t    </div>\n133\t  );\n134\t};\n135\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"73ead79b-bfaa-4007-bfdf-b04d598e97b4","timestamp":"2026-07-24T07:58:46.598Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T075643Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/packages/nextjs/components/scaffold-eth/Faucet.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { Address, AddressInput, Balance, EtherInput } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport { Address as AddressType, createWalletClient, http, parseEther } from \"viem\";\nimport { hardhat } from \"viem/chains\";\nimport { useAccount } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { BanknotesIcon } from \"@heroicons/react/24/outline\";\nimport { useTargetNetwork, useTransactor } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\n// Account index to use from generated hardhat accounts.\nconst FAUCET_ACCOUNT_INDEX = 0;\n\nconst localWalletClient = createWalletClient({\n  chain: hardhat,\n  transport: http(),\n});\n\n/**\n * Faucet modal which lets you send ETH to any address.\n */\nexport const Faucet = () => {\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);\n  const [inputAddress, setInputAddress] = useState<AddressType>();\n  const [faucetAddress, setFaucetAddress] = useState<AddressType>();\n  const [sendValue, setSendValue] = useState(\"\");\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n\n  const { chain: ConnectedChain } = useAccount();\n\n  const faucetTxn = useTransactor(localWalletClient);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const getFaucetAddress = async () => {\n      try {\n        const accounts = await localWalletClient.getAddresses();\n        setFaucetAddress(accounts[FAUCET_ACCOUNT_INDEX]);\n      } catch (error) {\n        notification.error(\n          <>\n            <p className=\"font-bold mt-0 mb-1\">Cannot connect to local provider</p>\n            <p className=\"m-0\">\n              - Did you forget to run <code className=\"italic bg-base-300 text-base font-bold\">yarn chain</code> ?\n            </p>\n            <p className=\"mt-1 break-normal\">\n              - Or you can change <code className=\"italic bg-base-300 text-base font-bold\">targetNetwork</code> in{\" \"}\n              <code className=\"italic bg-base-300 text-base font-bold\">scaffold.config.ts</code>\n            </p>\n          </>,\n        );\n        console.error(\"⚡️ ~ file: Faucet.tsx:getFaucetAddress ~ error\", error);\n      }\n    };\n    getFaucetAddress();\n  }, []);\n\n  const sendETH = async () => {\n    if (!faucetAddress || !inputAddress) {\n      return;\n    }\n    try {\n      setLoading(true);\n      await faucetTxn({\n        to: inputAddress,\n        value: parseEther(sendValue as `${number}`),\n        account: faucetAddress,\n      });\n      setLoading(false);\n      setInputAddress(undefined);\n      setSendValue(\"\");\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(\"⚡️ ~ file: Faucet.tsx:sendETH ~ error\", error);\n      setLoading(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  // Render only on local chain\n  if (ConnectedChain?.id !== hardhat.id) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div>\n      <label htmlFor=\"faucet-modal\" className=\"btn btn-primary btn-sm font-normal gap-1\">\n        <BanknotesIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n        <span>Faucet</span>\n      </label>\n      <input type=\"checkbox\" id=\"faucet-modal\" className=\"modal-toggle\" />\n      <label htmlFor=\"faucet-modal\" className=\"modal cursor-pointer\">\n        <label className=\"modal-box relative\">\n          {/* dummy input to capture event onclick on modal box */}\n          <input className=\"h-0 w-0 absolute top-0 left-0\" />\n          <h3 className=\"text-xl font-bold mb-3\">Local Faucet</h3>\n          <label htmlFor=\"faucet-modal\" className=\"btn btn-ghost btn-sm absolute right-3 top-3\">\n            ✕\n          </label>\n          <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n            <div className=\"flex space-x-4\">\n              <div>\n                <span className=\"text-sm font-bold\">From:</span>\n                <Address address={faucetAddress} onlyEnsOrAddress chain={targetNetwork} />\n              </div>\n              <div>\n                <span className=\"text-sm font-bold pl-3\">Available:</span>\n                <Balance address={faucetAddress} />\n              </div>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"flex flex-col space-y-3\">\n              <AddressInput\n                placeholder=\"Destination Address\"\n                value={inputAddress ?? \"\"}\n                onChange={value => setInputAddress(value as AddressType)}\n              />\n              <EtherInput\n                placeholder=\"Amount to send\"\n                onValueChange={({ valueInEth }) => setSendValue(valueInEth)}\n                style={{ width: \"100%\" }}\n              />\n              <button className=\"h-10 btn btn-primary btn-sm px-2\" onClick={sendETH} disabled={loading}>\n                {!loading ? (\n                  <BanknotesIcon className=\"h-6 w-6\" />\n                ) : (\n                  <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\"></span>\n                )}\n                <span>Send</span>\n              </button>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </label>\n      </label>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n","numLines":135,"startLine":1,"totalLines":135}}}

{ useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { MutateOptions } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\nimport { Abi, ExtractAbiFunctionNames } from \"abitype\";\nimport { Config, UseWriteContractParameters, useAccount, useConfig, useWriteContract } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { WriteContractErrorType, WriteContractReturnType } from \"wagmi/actions\";\nimport { WriteContractVariables } from \"wagmi/query\";\nimport { useSelectedNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { useDeployedContractInfo, useTransactor } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { AllowedChainIds, notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\nimport {\n  ContractAbi,\n  ContractName,\n  ScaffoldWriteContractOptions,\n  ScaffoldWriteContractVariables,\n  UseScaffoldWriteConfig,\n  simulateContractWriteAndNotifyError,\n} from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth/contract\";\n\ntype ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName extends ContractName> = Omit<\n  ReturnType<typeof useWriteContract>,\n  \"writeContract\" | \"writeContractAsync\"\n> & {\n  isMining: boolean;\n  writeContractAsync: <\n    TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">,\n  >(\n    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n    options?: ScaffoldWriteContractOptions,\n  ) => Promise<WriteContractReturnType | undefined>;\n  writeContract: <TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">>(\n    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n    options?: Omit<ScaffoldWriteContractOptions, \"onBlockConfirmation\" | \"blockConfirmations\">,\n  ) => void;\n};\n\nexport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n  config: UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName>,\n): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName>;\n/**\n * @deprecated Use object parameter version instead: useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"YourContract\" })\n */\nexport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n  contractName: TContractName,\n  writeContractParams?: UseWriteContractParameters,\n): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName>;\n\n/**\n * Wrapper around wagmi's useWriteContract hook which automatically loads (by name) the contract ABI and address from\n * the contracts present in deployedContracts.ts & externalContracts.ts corresponding to targetNetworks configured in scaffold.config.ts\n * @param contractName - name of the contract to be written to\n * @param config.chainId - optional chainId that is configured with the scaffold project to make use for multi-chain interactions.\n * @param writeContractParams - wagmi's useWriteContract parameters\n */\nexport function useScaffoldWriteContract<TContractName extends ContractName>(\n  configOrName: UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName> | TContractName,\n  writeContractParams?: UseWriteContractParameters,\n): ScaffoldWriteContractReturnType<TContractName> {\n  const finalConfig =\n    typeof configOrName === \"string\"\n      ? { contractName: configOrName, writeContractParams, chainId: undefined }\n      : (configOrName as UseScaffoldWriteConfig<TContractName>);\n  const { contractName, chainId, writeContractParams: finalWriteContractParams } = finalConfig;\n\n  const wagmiConfig = useConfig();\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (typeof configOrName === \"string\") {\n      console.warn(\n        \"Using `useScaffoldWriteContract` with a string parameter is deprecated. Please use the object parameter version instead.\",\n      );\n    }\n  }, [configOrName]);\n\n  const { chain: accountChain } = useAccount();\n  const writeTx = useTransactor();\n  const [isMining, setIsMining] = useState(false);\n\n  const wagmiContractWrite = useWriteContract(finalWriteContractParams);\n\n  const selectedNetwork = useSelectedNetwork(chainId);\n\n  const { data: deployedContractData } = useDeployedContractInfo({\n    contractName,\n    chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n  });\n\n  const sendContractWriteAsyncTx = async <\n    TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">,\n  >(\n    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n    options?: ScaffoldWriteContractOptions,\n  ) => {\n    if (!deployedContractData) {\n      notification.error(\"Target Contract is not deployed, did you forget to run `yarn deploy`?\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (!accountChain?.id) {\n      notification.error(\"Please connect your wallet\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (accountChain?.id !== selectedNetwork.id) {\n      notification.error(`Wallet is connected to the wrong network. Please switch to ${selectedNetwork.name}`);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    try {\n      setIsMining(true);\n      const { blockConfirmations, onBlockConfirmation, ...mutateOptions } = options || {};\n\n      const writeContractObject = {\n        abi: deployedContractData.abi as Abi,\n        address: deployedContractData.address,\n        ...variables,\n      } as WriteContractVariables<Abi, string, any[], Config, number>;\n\n      if (!finalConfig?.disableSimulate) {\n        await simulateContractWriteAndNotifyError({\n          wagmiConfig,\n          writeContractParams: writeContractObject,\n          chainId: selectedNetwork.id as AllowedChainIds,\n        });\n      }\n\n      const makeWriteWithParams = () =>\n        wagmiContractWrite.writeContractAsync(\n          writeContractObject,\n          mutateOptions as\n            | MutateOptions<\n                WriteContractReturnType,\n                WriteContractErrorType,\n                WriteContractVariables<Abi, string, any[], Config, number>,\n                unknown\n              >\n            | undefined,\n        );\n      const writeTxResult = await writeTx(makeWriteWithParams, { blockConfirmations, onBlockConfirmation });\n\n      return writeTxResult;\n    } catch (e: any) {\n      throw e;\n    } finally {\n      setIsMining(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const sendContractWriteTx = <\n    TContractName extends ContractName,\n    TFunctionName extends ExtractAbiFunctionNames<ContractAbi<TContractName>, \"nonpayable\" | \"payable\">,\n  >(\n    variables: ScaffoldWriteContractVariables<TContractName, TFunctionName>,\n    options?: Omit<ScaffoldWriteContractOptions, \"onBlockConfirmation\" | \"blockConfirmations\">,\n  ) => {\n    if (!deployedContractData) {\n      notification.error(\"Target Contract is not deployed, did you forget to run `yarn deploy`?\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if (!accountChain?.id) {\n      notification.error(\"Please connect your wallet\");\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (accountChain?.id !== selectedNetwork.id) {\n      notification.error(`Wallet is connected to the wrong network. Please switch to ${selectedNetwork.name}`);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    wagmiContractWrite.writeContract(\n      {\n        abi: deployedContractData.abi as Abi,\n        address: deployedContractData.address,\n        ...variables,\n      } as WriteContractVariables<Abi, string, any[], Config, number>,\n      options as\n        | MutateOptions<\n            WriteContractReturnType,\n            WriteContractErrorType,\n            WriteContractVariables<Abi, string, any[], Config, number>,\n            unknown\n          >\n        | undefined,\n    );\n  };\n\n  return {\n    ...wagmiContractWrite,\n    isMining,\n    // Overwrite wagmi's writeContactAsync\n    writeContractAsync: sendContractWriteAsyncTx,\n    // Overwrite wagmi's writeContract\n    writeContract: sendContractWriteTx,\n  };\n}\n","numLines":195,"startLine":1,"totalLines":195}}}

Balance props ===\nimport React, { CSSProperties } from \"react\";\nimport { Address, Chain } from \"viem\";\nexport type BalanceProps = {\n    address?: Address;\n    chain?: Chain;\n    defaultUsdMode?: boolean;\n    style?: CSSProperties;\n};\n/**\n * Balance Component\n *\n * Displays the balance of a given Ethereum address, with support for toggling between native token (e.g., ETH) and USD value.\n * - Fetches and displays the balance for the provided address and chain.\n * - Allows toggling between native token and USD display by clicking the component.\n * - Shows a loading skeleton while fetching, and an error message if fetching fails.\n *\n * @param {BalanceProps} props - The props for the Balance component.\n * @param {Address} [props.address] - (Optional) {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DBkrBRUpsJexG36mifiz4V","type":"tool_result","content":"=== BaseInput ===\nimport { ReactNode } from \"react\";\nimport { CommonInputProps } from \"./utils\";\nexport type BaseInputProps<T> = CommonInputProps<T> & {\n    error?: boolean;\n    prefix?: ReactNode;\n    suffix?: ReactNode;\n    reFocus?: boolean;\n};\n/**\n * BaseInput Component\n *\n * A flexible, styled input component used as the foundation for custom inputs (e.g., EtherInput, AddressInput).\n * - Supports prefix and suffix elements for icons or adornments.\n * - Handles error and disabled states with visual feedback.\n * - Can auto-focus and set cursor position at the end when `reFocus` is true (useful for programmatic focus).\n * - Accepts custom color classes for border, background, and text.\n *\n * @template T - The value type, must have a toString method (e.g., string, Address).\n * @param {BaseInputProps<T>} props - The props for the BaseInput component.\n * @param {string} [props.name] - (Optional) The name attribute for the input element.\n * @param {T} [props.value] - The value of the input.\n * @param {(value: T) => void} props.onChange - Callback fired when the input value changes.\n * @param {string} [props.placeholder] - (Optional) Placeholder text for the input.\n * @param {boolean} [props.error] - (Optional) If true, input is styled as error.\n * @param {boolean} [props.disabled] - (Optional) If true, the input is disabled.\n * @param {ReactNode} [props.prefix] - (Optional) Element to render before the input (e.g., icon).\n * @param {ReactNode} [props.suffix] - (Optional) Element to render after the input (e.g., button).\n * @param {boolean} [props.reFocus] - (Optional) If true, input auto-focuses and cursor moves to end.\n * @param {CSSProperties} [props.style] - (Optional) Custom CSS styles to apply to the component.\n *   Performance Warning: Always memoize style objects to prevent unnecessary re-renders.\n *\n * @example\n * <BaseInput\n *   name=\"username\"\n *   value={value}\n *   onChange={setValue}\n *   placeholder=\"Enter your name\"\n *   prefix={<UserIcon />}\n *   error={hasError}\n * />\n */\nexport declare const BaseInput: <T extends {\n    toString: () => string;\n} | undefined = string>({ name, value, onChange, placeholder, error, disabled, prefix, suffix, reFocus, style, }: BaseInputProps<T>) => import(\"react/jsx-runtime\").JSX.Element;\n//# sourceMappingURL=BaseInput.d.ts.map=== CommonInputProps ===\nimport { CSSProperties } from \"react\";\nexport type CommonInputProps<T = string> = {\n    value: T;\n    onChange: (newValue: T) => void;\n    name?: string;\n    placeholder?: string;\n    disabled?: boolean;\n    style?: CSSProperties;\n};\n//# sourceMappingURL=utils.d.ts.map=== Address props ===\nimport React, { CSSProperties } from \"react\";\nimport { Chain, type Address as AddressType } from \"viem\";\nexport type AddressProps = {\n    address?: AddressType;\n    disableAddressLink?: boolean;\n    format?: \"short\" | \"long\";\n    size?: \"xs\" | \"sm\" | \"base\" | \"lg\" | \"xl\" | \"2xl\" | \"3xl\";\n    onlyEnsOrAddress?: boolean;\n    chain?: Chain;\n    style?: CSSProperties;\n    blockExplorerAddressLink?: string;\n};\n/**\n * Address Component\n *\n * Displays an Ethereum address with ENS name resolution, avatar, and copy functionality.\n * - Resolves ENS names and displays ENS avatars when available.\n * - Shows a blockie (identicon) as fallback when no ENS avatar is available.\n * - Provides copy-to-clipboard functionality for the address.\n * - Supports linking to block explorers for address details.\n * - Displays loading skeletons while resolving ENS names.\n *\n * @param {AddressProps} props - The props for the Address component.\n * @param {AddressType} [props.address] - {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLXppz5QbqboMnLrAPku","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01QNrbCuoSXqd4LJd4XJV8Aq","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T075643Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/packages/hardhat/contracts/Staker.sol","content":"//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity 0.8.30;\n\nimport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\nimport {SafeERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\nimport {ReentrancyGuard} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\n\n/**\n * Minimal USDC staking vault.\n *\n * Users deposit USDC and can withdraw their full position at any time.\n * No rewards, no lockups, no admin. Internal accounting assumes a\n * standard (non fee-on-transfer, non rebasing) token — which USDC is.\n */\ncontract Staker is ReentrancyGuard {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    /// Staked amount per user, in USDC base units (6 decimals).\n    mapping(address => uint256) public stakedBalance;\n\n    /// Total USDC currently staked across all users.\n    uint256 public totalStaked;\n\n    event Staked(address indexed user, uint256 amount);\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed user, uint256 amount);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error NothingStaked();\n\n    constructor(IERC20 _usdc) {\n        require(address(_usdc) != address(0), \"usdc is zero address\");\n        usdc = _usdc;\n    }\n\n    /// Deposit `amount` USDC. Requires prior approval on the USDC contract.\n    function stake(uint256 amount) external nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        stakedBalance[msg.sender] += amount;\n        totalStaked += amount;\n\n        usdc.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        emit Staked(msg.sender, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// Withdraw the caller's entire staked position.\n    function withdraw() external nonReentrant {\n        uint256 amount = stakedBalance[msg.sender];\n        if (amount == 0) revert NothingStaked();\n\n        stakedBalance[msg.sender] = 0;\n        totalStaked -= amount;\n\n        usdc.safeTransfer(msg.sender, amount);\n\n        emit Withdrawn(msg.sender, amount);\n    }\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":356,"cache_read_input_tokens":73136,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":356},"output_tokens":56,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"8f0951df-3e85-448e-aa7e-835bc7ba33c0","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:02:11.750Z","request_id":"req_011CdLXpkoZE3Z1Wbfkru8vA"}

Internal accounting assumes a\n * standard (non fee-on-transfer, non rebasing) token — which USDC is.\n */\ncontract Staker is ReentrancyGuard {\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n\n    /// Staked amount per user, in USDC base units (6 decimals).\n    mapping(address => uint256) public stakedBalance;\n\n    /// Total USDC currently staked across all users.\n    uint256 public totalStaked;\n\n    event Staked(address indexed user, uint256 amount);\n    event Withdrawn(address indexed user, uint256 amount);\n\n    error ZeroAmount();\n    error NothingStaked();\n\n    constructor(IERC20 _usdc) {\n        require(address(_usdc) != address(0), \"usdc is zero address\");\n        usdc = _usdc;\n    }\n\n    /// Deposit `amount` USDC. Requires prior approval on the USDC contract.\n    function stake(uint256 amount) external nonReentrant {\n        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n\n        stakedBalance[msg.sender] += amount;\n        totalStaked += amount;\n\n        usdc.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n\n        emit Staked(msg.sender, amount);\n    }\n\n    /// Withdraw the caller's entire staked position.\n    function withdraw() external nonReentrant {\n        uint256 amount = stakedBalance[msg.sender];\n        if (amount == 0) revert NothingStaked();\n\n        stakedBalance[msg.sender] = 0;\n        totalStaked -= amount;\n\n        usdc.safeTransfer(msg.sender, amount);\n\n        emit Withdrawn(msg.sender, amount);\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLXrGR4PUGxw4zzQWfn1","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now the deploy script:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":736,"cache_read_input_tokens":74577,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":736},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"ff68f915-0a2b-4502-a8cd-cc2ed1d180e0","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:02:21.725Z","request_id":"req_011CdLXrEs3NiYGcZFByTJ4Y"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLXrGR4PUGxw4zzQWfn1","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EcuFbJyoPqyuB39Y5rfXmm","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T075643Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/packages/hardhat/deploy/00_deploy_staker.ts","content":"import { deployScript, artifacts } from \"../rocketh/deploy.js\";\n\n/**\n * Real USDC token addresses by chainId. On these networks the Staker is wired\n * to the production token instead of deploying a mock.\n */\nconst USDC_ADDRESSES: Record<number, `0x${string}`> = {\n  8453: \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\", // Base mainnet\n  84532: \"0x036CbD53842c5426634e7929541eC2318f3dCF7e\", // Base Sepolia (Circle test USDC)\n};\n\n/**\n * Deploys the Staker contract wired to USDC.\n *\n * - On Base / Base Sepolia: uses the real USDC token.\n * - On any other network (e.g. local hardhat): deploys a faithful 6-decimal\n *   MockUSDC, registers it as \"USDC\" for the frontend, and seeds the deployer.\n */\nexport default deployScript(\n  async env => {\n    const { deployer } = env.namedAccounts;\n    const chainId = env.network.chain.id;\n\n    let usdcAddress = USDC_ADDRESSES[chainId];\n\n    if (!usdcAddress) {\n      const mockUsdc = await env.deploy(\"USDC\", {\n        account: deployer,\n        artifact: artifacts.MockUSDC,\n        args: [\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\"],\n      });\n      usdcAddress = mockUsdc.address;\n\n      // Seed the deployer with 1,000,000 USDC (6 decimals) for local testing.\n      await env.execute(mockUsdc, {\n        account: deployer,\n        functionName: \"mint\",\n        args: [deployer, 1_000_000_000_000n],\n      });\n      console.log(\"🪙 Deployed MockUSDC at\", usdcAddress);\n    } else {\n      console.log(\"🪙 Using real USDC at\", usdcAddress);\n    }\n\n    const staker = await env.deploy(\"Staker\", {\n      account: deployer,\n      artifact: artifacts.Staker,\n      args: [usdcAddress],\n    });\n    console.log(\"🏦 Deployed Staker at\", staker.address);\n  },\n  {\n    tags: [\"Staker\", \"USDC\"],\n  },\n);\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":736,"cache_read_input_tokens":74577,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":736},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"34fbe9bd-6943-47f8-b854-0aa5c6f04da5","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:02:31.940Z","request_id":"req_011CdLXrEs3NiYGcZFByTJ4Y"}

On these networks the Staker is wired\n * to the production token instead of deploying a mock.\n */\nconst USDC_ADDRESSES: Record<number, `0x${string}`> = {\n  8453: \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\", // Base mainnet\n  84532: \"0x036CbD53842c5426634e7929541eC2318f3dCF7e\", // Base Sepolia (Circle test USDC)\n};\n\n/**\n * Deploys the Staker contract wired to USDC.\n *\n * - On Base / Base Sepolia: uses the real USDC token.\n * - On any other network (e.g. local hardhat): deploys a faithful 6-decimal\n *   MockUSDC, registers it as \"USDC\" for the frontend, and seeds the deployer.\n */\nexport default deployScript(\n  async env => {\n    const { deployer } = env.namedAccounts;\n    const chainId = env.network.chain.id;\n\n    let usdcAddress = USDC_ADDRESSES[chainId];\n\n    if (!usdcAddress) {\n      const mockUsdc = await env.deploy(\"USDC\", {\n        account: deployer,\n        artifact: artifacts.MockUSDC,\n        args: [\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\"],\n      });\n      usdcAddress = mockUsdc.address;\n\n      // Seed the deployer with 1,000,000 USDC (6 decimals) for local testing.\n      await env.execute(mockUsdc, {\n        account: deployer,\n        functionName: \"mint\",\n        args: [deployer, 1_000_000_000_000n],\n      });\n      console.log(\"🪙 Deployed MockUSDC at\", usdcAddress);\n    } else {\n      console.log(\"🪙 Using real USDC at\", usdcAddress);\n    }\n\n    const staker = await env.deploy(\"Staker\", {\n      account: deployer,\n      artifact: artifacts.Staker,\n      args: [usdcAddress],\n    });\n    console.log(\"🏦 Deployed Staker at\", staker.address);\n  },\n  {\n    tags: [\"Staker\", \"USDC\"],\n  },\n);\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLXtp29dFSEUHyVLkD7B","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01XJKffvzeP6zkXvCwnAuLAo","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T075643Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/packages/hardhat/test/Staker.ts","content":"import { expect } from \"chai\";\nimport { network } from \"hardhat\";\nimport type { Abi_Staker } from \"../generated/abis/Staker.js\";\nimport type { Abi_MockUSDC } from \"../generated/abis/MockUSDC.js\";\nimport { loadAndExecuteDeploymentsFromFiles } from \"../rocketh/environment.js\";\n\nconst { provider, networkHelpers, ethers } = await network.create();\n\nconst usdc = (n: string) => ethers.parseUnits(n, 6);\n\nasync function deployFixture() {\n  const env = await loadAndExecuteDeploymentsFromFiles({ provider });\n\n  const stakerDeployment = env.get<Abi_Staker>(\"Staker\");\n  const usdcDeployment = env.get<Abi_MockUSDC>(\"USDC\");\n\n  const staker = await ethers.getContractAt(stakerDeployment.abi, stakerDeployment.address);\n  const token = await ethers.getContractAt(usdcDeployment.abi, usdcDeployment.address);\n\n  const [deployer, alice] = await ethers.getSigners();\n  // deployer is seeded with 1,000,000 USDC by the deploy script. Give alice some.\n  await token.transfer(alice.address, usdc(\"1000\"));\n\n  return { staker, token, deployer, alice };\n}\n\ndescribe(\"Staker\", function () {\n  it(\"Uses a 6-decimal USDC token\", async function () {\n    const { token } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    expect(await token.decimals()).to.equal(6);\n    expect(await token.symbol()).to.equal(\"USDC\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"Wires the Staker to the USDC address\", async function () {\n    const { staker, token } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    expect(await staker.usdc()).to.equal(await token.getAddress());\n  });\n\n  it(\"Stakes USDC after approval and tracks the position\", async function () {\n    const { staker, token, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddress = await staker.getAddress();\n\n    await token.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddress, usdc(\"250\"));\n    await expect(staker.connect(alice).stake(usdc(\"250\")))\n      .to.emit(staker, \"Staked\")\n      .withArgs(alice.address, usdc(\"250\"));\n\n    expect(await staker.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(usdc(\"250\"));\n    expect(await staker.totalStaked()).to.equal(usdc(\"250\"));\n    expect(await token.balanceOf(stakerAddress)).to.equal(usdc(\"250\"));\n    expect(await token.balanceOf(alice.address)).to.equal(usdc(\"750\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"Withdraws the full position\", async function () {\n    const { staker, token, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddress = await staker.getAddress();\n\n    await token.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddress, usdc(\"250\"));\n    await staker.connect(alice).stake(usdc(\"250\"));\n\n    await expect(staker.connect(alice).withdraw())\n      .to.emit(staker, \"Withdrawn\")\n      .withArgs(alice.address, usdc(\"250\"));\n\n    expect(await staker.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(0n);\n    expect(await staker.totalStaked()).to.equal(0n);\n    expect(await token.balanceOf(alice.address)).to.equal(usdc(\"1000\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"Accumulates across multiple stakes\", async function () {\n    const { staker, token, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddress = await staker.getAddress();\n\n    await token.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddress, usdc(\"300\"));\n    await staker.connect(alice).stake(usdc(\"100\"));\n    await staker.connect(alice).stake(usdc(\"200\"));\n\n    expect(await staker.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(usdc(\"300\"));\n  });\n\n  it(\"Reverts when staking zero\", async function () {\n    const { staker, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    await expect(staker.connect(alice).stake(0n)).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(staker, \"ZeroAmount\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"Reverts when withdrawing with no position\", async function () {\n    const { staker, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    await expect(staker.connect(alice).withdraw()).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(staker, \"NothingStaked\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"Keeps user positions independent\", async function () {\n    const { staker, token, deployer, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n    const stakerAddress = await staker.getAddress();\n\n    await token.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddress, usdc(\"400\"));\n    await staker.connect(alice).stake(usdc(\"400\"));\n\n    await token.connect(deployer).approve(stakerAddress, usdc(\"100\"));\n    await staker.connect(deployer).stake(usdc(\"100\"));\n\n    expect(await staker.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(usdc(\"400\"));\n    expect(await staker.stakedBalance(deployer.address)).to.equal(usdc(\"100\"));\n    expect(await staker.totalStaked()).to.equal(usdc(\"500\"));\n\n    await staker.connect(alice).withdraw();\n    expect(await staker.stakedBalance(deployer.address)).to.equal(usdc(\"100\"));\n    expect(await staker.totalStaked()).to.equal(usdc(\"100\"));\n  });\n});\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1234,"cache_read_input_tokens":77069,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1234},"output_tokens":42,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"5322d3b1-9be0-4b62-a2f2-f57b3d245c06","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:03:15.719Z","request_id":"req_011CdLXtn3LWJPJBpinmeenY"}

staker.stakedBalance(deployer.address)).to.equal(usdc(\"100\"));\n    expect(await staker.totalStaked()).to.equal(usdc(\"500\"));\n\n    await staker.connect(alice).withdraw();\n    expect(await staker.stakedBalance(deployer.address)).to.equal(usdc(\"100\"));\n    expect(await staker.totalStaked()).to.equal(usdc(\"100\"));\n  });\n});\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ATntuMvcHhgL84G5mbuEAt","type":"tool_result","content":"40\t\n41\t  const { query: queryOptions, watch, ...readContractConfig } = readConfig;\n42\t  // set watch to true by default\n43\t  const defaultWatch = watch ?? true;\n44\t\n45\t  const readContractHookRes = useReadContract({\n46\t    chainId: selectedNetwork.id,\n47\t    functionName,\n48\t    address: deployedContract?.address,\n49\t    abi: deployedContract?.abi,\n50\t    args,\n51\t    ...(readContractConfig as any),\n52\t    query: {\n53\t      enabled: !Array.isArray(args) || !args.some(arg => arg === undefined),\n54\t      ...queryOptions,\n55\t    },\n56\t  }) as Omit<ReturnType<typeof useReadContract>, \"data\" | \"refetch\"> & {\n57\t    data: AbiFunctionReturnType<ContractAbi, TFunctionName> | undefined;\n58\t    refetch: (\n59\t      options?: RefetchOptions | undefined,\n60\t    ) => Promise<QueryObserverResult<AbiFunctionReturnType<ContractAbi, TFunctionName>, ReadContractErrorType>>;\n61\t  };\n62\t\n63\t  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n64\t  const { data: blockNumber } = useBlockNumber({\n65\t    watch: defaultWatch,\n66\t    chainId: selectedNetwork.id,\n67\t    query: {\n68\t      enabled: defaultWatch,\n69\t    },\n70\t  });\n71\t\n72\t  useEffect(() => {\n73\t    if (defaultWatch) {\n74\t      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: readContractHookRes.queryKey });\n75\t    }\n76\t    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n77\t  }, [blockNumber]);\n78\t\n79\t  return readContractHookRes;\n80\t};\n81\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"5c5ddbc8-e376-40dd-879c-d63b0c4f5978","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:04:48.347Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T075643Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/packages/nextjs/hooks/scaffold-eth/useScaffoldReadContract.ts","content":"\n  const { query: queryOptions, watch, ...readContractConfig } = readConfig;\n  // set watch to true by default\n  const defaultWatch = watch ?? true;\n\n  const readContractHookRes = useReadContract({\n    chainId: selectedNetwork.id,\n    functionName,\n    address: deployedContract?.address,\n    abi: deployedContract?.abi,\n    args,\n    ...(readContractConfig as any),\n    query: {\n      enabled: !Array.isArray(args) || !args.some(arg => arg === undefined),\n      ...queryOptions,\n    },\n  }) as Omit<ReturnType<typeof useReadContract>, \"data\" | \"refetch\"> & {\n    data: AbiFunctionReturnType<ContractAbi, TFunctionName> | undefined;\n    refetch: (\n      options?: RefetchOptions | undefined,\n    ) => Promise<QueryObserverResult<AbiFunctionReturnType<ContractAbi, TFunctionName>, ReadContractErrorType>>;\n  };\n\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n  const { data: blockNumber } = useBlockNumber({\n    watch: defaultWatch,\n    chainId: selectedNetwork.id,\n    query: {\n      enabled: defaultWatch,\n    },\n  });\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (defaultWatch) {\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: readContractHookRes.queryKey });\n    }\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [blockNumber]);\n\n  return readContractHookRes;\n};\n","numLines":42,"startLine":40,"totalLines":81}}}

WrongNetworkDropdown } from \"./WrongNetworkDropdown\";\n8\timport { ConnectButton } from \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit\";\n9\timport { Balance } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\n10\timport { \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth/useTargetNetwork\";\n14\t\n15\t/**\n16\t * Custom Wagmi Connect Button (watch balance + custom design)\n17\t */\n18\texport const RainbowKitCustomConnectButton = () => {\n19\t  const networkColor = useNetworkColor();\n20\t  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n21\t\n22\t  return (\n23\t    <ConnectButton.Custom>\n24\t      {({ account, chain, openConnectModal, mounted }) => {\n25\t        const connected = mounted && account && chain;\n26\t        const blockExplorerAddressLink = account\n27\t          ? getBlockExplorerAddressLink(targetNetwork, account.address)\n28\t          : undefined;\n29\t\n30\t        return (\n31\t          <>\n32\t            {(() => {\n33\t              if (!connected) {\n34\t                return (\n35\t                  <button className=\"btn btn-primary btn-sm\" onClick={openConnectModal} type=\"button\">\n36\t                    Connect Wallet\n37\t                  </button>\n38\t                );\n39\t              }\n40\t\n41\t              if (chain.unsupported || chain.id !== targetNetwork.id) {\n42\t                return <WrongNetworkDropdown />;\n43\t              }\n44\t\n45\t              return (\n46\t                <>\n47\t                  <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center mr-2\">\n48\t                    <Balance\n49\t                      address={account.address as Address}\n50\t                      style={{\n51\t                        minHeight: \"0\",\n52\t                        height: \"auto\",\n53\t                        fontSize: \"0.8em\",\n54\t                      }}\n55\t                    />\n56\t                    <span className=\"text-xs\" style={{ color: networkColor }}>\n57\t                      {chain.name}\n58\t                    </span>\n59\t                  </div>\n60\t                  <AddressInfoDropdown\n61\t                    address={account.address as Address}\n62\t                    displayName={account.displayName}\n63\t                    ensAvatar={account.ensAvatar}\n64\t                    blockExplorerAddressLink={blockExplorerAddressLink}\n65\t                  />\n66\t                  <AddressQRCodeModal address={account.address as Address} modalId=\"qrcode-modal\" />\n67\t                  <RevealBurnerPKModal />\n68\t                </>\n69\t              );\n70\t );\n76\t};\n77\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"03844eca-8205-48e6-b833-ebe3f9a628f5","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:05:09.122Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T075643Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/packages/nextjs/components/scaffold-eth/RainbowKitCustomConnectButton/index.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\n// @refresh reset\nimport { AddressInfoDropdown } from \"./AddressInfoDropdown\";\nimport { AddressQRCodeModal } from \"./AddressQRCodeModal\";\nimport { RevealBurnerPKModal } from \"./RevealBurnerPKModal\";\nimport { WrongNetworkDropdown } from \"./WrongNetworkDropdown\";\nimport { ConnectButton } from \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit\";\nimport { Balance } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport { getBlockExplorerAddressLink } from \"@scaffold-ui/hooks\";\nimport { Address } from \"viem\";\nimport { useNetworkColor } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { useTargetNetwork } from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth/useTargetNetwork\";\n\n/**\n * Custom Wagmi Connect Button (watch balance + custom design)\n */\nexport const RainbowKitCustomConnectButton = () => {\n  const networkColor = useNetworkColor();\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n\n  return (\n    <ConnectButton.Custom>\n      {({ account, chain, openConnectModal, mounted }) => {\n        const connected = mounted && account && chain;\n        const blockExplorerAddressLink = account\n          ? getBlockExplorerAddressLink(targetNetwork, account.address)\n          : undefined;\n\n        return (\n          <>\n            {(() => {\n              if (!connected) {\n                return (\n                  <button className=\"btn btn-primary btn-sm\" onClick={openConnectModal} type=\"button\">\n                    Connect Wallet\n                  </button>\n                );\n              }\n\n              if (chain.unsupported || chain.id !== targetNetwork.id) {\n                return <WrongNetworkDropdown />;\n              }\n\n              return (\n                <>\n                  <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center mr-2\">\n                    <Balance\n                      address={account.address as Address}\n                      style={{\n                        minHeight: \"0\",\n                        height: \"auto\",\n                        fontSize: \"0.8em\",\n                      }}\n                    />\n                    <span className=\"text-xs\" style={{ color: networkColor }}>\n                      {chain.name}\n                    </span>\n                  </div>\n                  <AddressInfoDropdown\n                    address={account.address as Address}\n                    displayName={account.displayName}\n                    ensAvatar={account.ensAvatar}\n                    blockExplorerAddressLink={blockExplorerAddressLink}\n                  />\n                  <AddressQRCodeModal address={account.address as Address} modalId=\"qrcode-modal\" />\n                  <RevealBurnerPKModal />\n                </>\n              );\n            })()}\n          </>\n        );\n      }}\n    </ConnectButton.Custom>\n  );\n};\n","numLines":77,"startLine":1,"totalLines":77}}}
