{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLY4p695jMa91sRVuYja","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_017Q8eJdgfxmdE9bWqGL9W78","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T075643Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/packages/nextjs/app/stake/page.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { useConnectModal } from \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit\";\nimport { Address, Balance } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount, useSwitchChain } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { ArrowDownTrayIcon, BanknotesIcon, WalletIcon } from \"@heroicons/react/24/outline\";\nimport {\n  useDeployedContractInfo,\n  useScaffoldReadContract,\n  useScaffoldWriteContract,\n  useTargetNetwork,\n} from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { getParsedError, notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst usd = new Intl.NumberFormat(\"en-US\", { style: \"currency\", currency: \"USD\" });\n\n// USDC is a dollar stablecoin, so token units map ~1:1 to USD.\nconst formatToken = (value: bigint | undefined, decimals: number) =>\n  value === undefined\n    ? \"—\"\n    : Number(formatUnits(value, decimals)).toLocaleString(\"en-US\", {\n        minimumFractionDigits: 2,\n        maximumFractionDigits: 2,\n      });\n\nconst formatUsd = (value: bigint | undefined, decimals: number) =>\n  value === undefined ? \"\" : usd.format(Number(formatUnits(value, decimals)));\n\nconst Stake: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address, chain } = useAccount();\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const { openConnectModal } = useConnectModal();\n  const { switchChain, isPending: isSwitching } = useSwitchChain();\n\n  const { data: stakerContract } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"Staker\" });\n  const stakerAddress = stakerContract?.address;\n\n  const { data: usdcDecimals } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"USDC\", functionName: \"decimals\" });\n  const decimals = usdcDecimals ?? 6;\n\n  const { data: walletBalance, refetch: refetchWallet } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [address],\n  });\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [address, stakerAddress],\n  });\n  const { data: staked, refetch: refetchStaked } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"Staker\",\n    functionName: \"stakedBalance\",\n    args: [address],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeStaker } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"Staker\" });\n\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [isApproving, setIsApproving] = useState(false);\n  const [approveCooldown, setApproveCooldown] = useState(false);\n  const [isStaking, setIsStaking] = useState(false);\n  const [isWithdrawing, setIsWithdrawing] = useState(false);\n  const [txError, setTxError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  // Parse the input safely — parseUnits throws on malformed values.\n  let parsedAmount: bigint | null = null;\n  try {\n    parsedAmount = amount ? parseUnits(amount, decimals) : null;\n  } catch {\n    parsedAmount = null;\n  }\n\n  const hasAmount = parsedAmount !== null && parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const exceedsBalance = hasAmount && walletBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount! > walletBalance;\n  const needsApproval = hasAmount && !exceedsBalance && allowance !== undefined && allowance < parsedAmount!;\n  const wrongNetwork = !!chain && chain.id !== targetNetwork.id;\n\n  const handleMax = () => {\n    if (walletBalance !== undefined) setAmount(formatUnits(walletBalance, decimals));\n  };\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!parsedAmount || !stakerAddress) return;\n    setTxError(null);\n    try {\n      setIsApproving(true);\n      await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [stakerAddress, parsedAmount] });\n      // Cover the confirmation → RPC-cache gap: keep the button locked while the\n      // fresh allowance propagates so the user can't double-submit.\n      setApproveCooldown(true);\n      await refetchAllowance();\n      setTimeout(() => setApproveCooldown(false), 4000);\n    } catch (e) {\n      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setIsApproving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleStake = async () => {\n    if (!parsedAmount) return;\n    setTxError(null);\n    try {\n      setIsStaking(true);\n      await writeStaker({ functionName: \"stake\", args: [parsedAmount] });\n      notification.success(\"Staked successfully\");\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      await Promise.all([refetchWallet(), refetchStaked(), refetchAllowance()]);\n    } catch (e) {\n      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setIsStaking(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleWithdraw = async () => {\n    setTxError(null);\n    try {\n      setIsWithdrawing(true);\n      await writeStaker({ functionName: \"withdraw\" });\n      notification.success(\"Withdrew your full position\");\n      await Promise.all([refetchWallet(), refetchStaked()]);\n    } catch (e) {\n      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setIsWithdrawing(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const renderPrimaryAction = () => {\n    if (!address) {\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\" onClick={openConnectModal} type=\"button\">\n          Connect Wallet\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    if (wrongNetwork) {\n      return (\n        <button\n          className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\"\n          disabled={isSwitching}\n          onClick={() => switchChain({ chainId: targetNetwork.id })}\n          type=\"button\"\n        >\n          {isSwitching ? (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Switching…\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            `Switch to ${targetNetwork.name}`\n          )}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    if (!stakerAddress) {\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn w-full\" disabled type=\"button\">\n          Staking not available on {chain?.name ?? \"this network\"}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    if (needsApproval) {\n      const busy = isApproving || approveCooldown;\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\" disabled={busy} onClick={handleApprove} type=\"button\">\n          {isApproving ? (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Approving…\n            </>\n          ) : approveCooldown ? (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Confirming…\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            \"Approve USDC\"\n          )}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    return (\n      <button\n        className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\"\n        disabled={!hasAmount || exceedsBalance || isStaking}\n        onClick={handleStake}\n        type=\"button\"\n      >\n        {isStaking ? units map ~1:1 to USD.\nconst formatToken = (value: bigint | undefined, decimals: number) =>\n  value === undefined\n    ? \"—\"\n    : Number(formatUnits(value, decimals)).toLocaleString(\"en-US\", {\n        minimumFractionDigits: 2,\n        maximumFractionDigits: 2,\n      });\n\nconst formatUsd = (value: bigint | undefined, decimals: number) =>\n  value === undefined ? \"\" : });\n  const stakerAddress = stakerContract?.address;\n\n  const { data: usdcDecimals } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"USDC\", functionName: \"decimals\" });\n  const decimals = const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [address, stakerAddress],\n  });\n  const { data: staked, refetch: refetchStaked } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"Staker\",\n    functionName: \"stakedBalance\",\n    args: [address],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeStaker } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"Staker\" });\n\n  const [amount, = parsedAmount !== null && parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const exceedsBalance = hasAmount && walletBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount! > walletBalance;\n  const needsApproval = hasAmount && !exceedsBalance && allowance !== undefined && allowance < parsedAmount!;\n  const wrongNetwork = !!chain && chain.id !== targetNetwork.id;\n\n  const handleMax = () => {\n    if (walletBalance !== undefined) setIsApproving(true);\n      await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [stakerAddress, parsedAmount] });\n      // Cover the confirmation → RPC-cache gap: keep the button locked while the\n      // fresh allowance propagates so the user can't double-submit.\n      setApproveCooldown(true);\n      await refetchAllowance();\n      setTimeout(() => setApproveCooldown(false), 4000);\n    } catch (e) {\n      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setIsApproving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleStake = async () => {\n    if (!parsedAmount) return;\n    setTxError(null);\n    try {\n      setIsStaking(true);\n      await writeStaker({ functionName: \"stake\", args: [parsedAmount] });\n      notification.success(\"Staked successfully\");\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      await Promise.all([refetchWallet(), refetchStaked(), refetchAllowance()]);\n    } catch (e) {\n      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setIsStaking(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleWithdraw = async () => {\n    setTxError(null);\n    try {\n      setIsWithdrawing(true);\n      await writeStaker({ functionName: \"withdraw\" });\n      notification.success(\"Withdrew your full position\");\n      await Promise.all([refetchWallet(), refetchStaked()]);\n    } catch (e) {\n      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setIsWithdrawing(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const renderPrimaryAction = () => {\n    if (!address) {\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\" onClick={openConnectModal} (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Switching…\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            `Switch to ${targetNetwork.name}`\n          )}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    if (!stakerAddress) {\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn w-full\" disabled type=\"button\">\n          Staking not available on {chain?.name ?? \"this network\"}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    if (needsApproval) {\n      const busy = isApproving || approveCooldown;\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\" disabled={busy} onClick={handleApprove} type=\"button\">\n          {isApproving ? {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01LfZgNzK3tQnCUNWupwaZhz","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n2\tpragma solidity 0.8.30;\n3\t\n4\timport {IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol\";\n5\timport {SafeERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\n6\timport {ReentrancyGuard} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\n7\t\n8\t/**\n9\t * Minimal USDC staking vault.\n10\t *\n11\t * Users deposit USDC and can withdraw their full position at any time.\n12\t * No rewards, no lockups, no admin. Internal accounting assumes a\n13\t * standard (non fee-on-transfer, non rebasing) token — which USDC is.\n14\t */\n15\tcontract Staker is ReentrancyGuard {\n16\t    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n17\t\n18\t    IERC20 public immutable usdc;\n19\t\n20\t    /// Staked amount per user, in USDC base units (6 decimals).\n21\t    mapping(address => uint256) public stakedBalance;\n22\t\n23\t    /// Total USDC currently staked across all users.\n24\t    uint256 public totalStaked;\n25\t\n26\t    event Staked(address indexed user, uint256 amount);\n27\t    event Withdrawn(address indexed user, uint256 amount);\n28\t\n29\t    error ZeroAmount();\n30\t    error NothingStaked();\n31\t\n32\t    constructor(IERC20 _usdc) {\n33\t        require(address(_usdc) != address(0), \"usdc is zero address\");\n34\t        usdc = _usdc;\n35\t    }\n36\t\n37\t    /// Deposit `amount` USDC. Requires prior approval on the USDC contract.\n38\t    function stake(uint256 amount) external nonReentrant {\n39\t        if (amount == 0) revert ZeroAmount();\n40\t\n41\t        stakedBalance[msg.sender] += amount;\n42\t        totalStaked += amount;\n43\t\n44\t        usdc.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);\n45\t\n46\t        emit Staked(msg.sender, amount);\n47\t    }\n48\t\n49\t    /// Withdraw the caller's entire staked position.\n50\t    function withdraw() external nonReentrant {\n51\t        uint256 amount = stakedBalance[msg.sender];\n52\t        if (amount == 0) revert NothingStaked();\n53\t\n54\t        stakedBalance[msg.sender] = 0;\n55\t        totalStaked -= amount;\n56\t\n57\t        usdc.safeTransfer(msg.sender, amount);\n58\t\n59\t        emit Withdrawn(msg.sender, amount);\n60\t    }\n61\t}\n62\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_016HW7cpP9otX9FZSLdnUMc9","session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"04ab2a8e-1099-4b9b-bb90-83bb3f5722e6","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:09:35.892Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review staking dApp code"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019TshHEwCrYvgaEUWpUKmdk","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n2\tpragma solidity 0.8.30;\n3\t\n4\timport {ERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol\";\n5\t\n6\t/**\n7\t * Local-development stand-in for USDC.\n8\t *\n9\t * Kept faithful to the real token where it matters for a frontend:\n10\t * 6 decimals, \"USD Coin\" / \"USDC\" metadata, standard ERC20 semantics.\n11\t * Adds an open `mint` so developers can fund test accounts. Do NOT deploy\n12\t * to a live network — the real USDC (0x8335...) is used there.\n13\t */\n14\tcontract MockUSDC is ERC20 {\n15\t    constructor(string memory name_, string memory symbol_) ERC20(name_, symbol_) {}\n16\t\n17\t    function decimals() public pure override returns (uint8) {\n18\t        return 6;\n19\t    }\n20\t\n21\t    function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {\n22\t        _mint(to, amount);\n23\t    }\n24\t}\n25\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_016HW7cpP9otX9FZSLdnUMc9","session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"77936d8e-d520-48fa-811e-7b5355d403cf","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:09:36.897Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review staking dApp code"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011Bcs5Bufewkq9GjD6FUGfL","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { deployScript, artifacts } from \"../rocketh/deploy.js\";\n2\t\n3\t/**\n4\t * Real USDC token addresses by chainId. On these networks the Staker is wired\n5\t * to the production token instead of deploying a mock.\n6\t */\n7\tconst USDC_ADDRESSES: Record<number, `0x${string}`> = {\n8\t  8453: \"0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913\", // Base mainnet\n9\t  84532: \"0x036CbD53842c5426634e7929541eC2318f3dCF7e\", // Base Sepolia (Circle test USDC)\n10\t};\n11\t\n12\t/**\n13\t * Deploys the Staker contract wired to USDC.\n14\t *\n15\t * - On Base / Base Sepolia: uses the real USDC token.\n16\t * - On any other network (e.g. local hardhat): deploys a faithful 6-decimal\n17\t *   MockUSDC, registers it as \"USDC\" for the frontend, and seeds the deployer.\n18\t */\n19\texport default deployScript(\n20\t  async env => {\n21\t    const { deployer } = env.namedAccounts;\n22\t    const chainId = env.network.chain.id;\n23\t\n24\t    let usdcAddress = USDC_ADDRESSES[chainId];\n25\t\n26\t    if (!usdcAddress) {\n27\t      const mockUsdc = await env.deploy(\"USDC\", {\n28\t        account: deployer,\n29\t        artifact: artifacts.MockUSDC,\n30\t        args: [\"USD Coin\", \"USDC\"],\n31\t      });\n32\t      usdcAddress = mockUsdc.address;\n33\t\n34\t      // Seed the deployer with 1,000,000 USDC (6 decimals) for local testing.\n35\t      await env.execute(mockUsdc, {\n36\t        account: deployer,\n37\t        functionName: \"mint\",\n38\t        args: [deployer, 1_000_000_000_000n],\n39\t      });\n40\t      console.log(\"🪙 Deployed MockUSDC at\", usdcAddress);\n41\t    } else {\n42\t      console.log(\"🪙 Using real USDC at\", usdcAddress);\n43\t    }\n44\t\n45\t    const staker = await env.deploy(\"Staker\", {\n46\t      account: deployer,\n47\t      artifact: artifacts.Staker,\n48\t      args: [usdcAddress],\n49\t    });\n50\t    console.log(\"🏦 Deployed Staker at\", staker.address);\n51\t  },\n52\t  {\n53\t    tags: [\"Staker\", \"USDC\"],\n54\t  },\n55\t);\n56\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_016HW7cpP9otX9FZSLdnUMc9","session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"43b85312-f187-4e8c-96a9-43af514af828","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:09:37.914Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review staking dApp code"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SRjBG4vBCn2xLSobBpTDBJ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { expect } from \"chai\";\n2\timport { network } from \"hardhat\";\n3\timport type { Abi_Staker } from \"../generated/abis/Staker.js\";\n4\timport type { Abi_MockUSDC } from \"../generated/abis/MockUSDC.js\";\n5\timport { loadAndExecuteDeploymentsFromFiles } from \"../rocketh/environment.js\";\n6\t\n7\tconst { provider, networkHelpers, ethers } = await network.create();\n8\t\n9\tconst usdc = (n: string) => ethers.parseUnits(n, 6);\n10\t\n11\tasync function deployFixture() {\n12\t  const env = await loadAndExecuteDeploymentsFromFiles({ provider });\n13\t\n14\t  const stakerDeployment = env.get<Abi_Staker>(\"Staker\");\n15\t  const usdcDeployment = env.get<Abi_MockUSDC>(\"USDC\");\n16\t\n17\t  const staker = await ethers.getContractAt(stakerDeployment.abi, stakerDeployment.address);\n18\t  const token = await ethers.getContractAt(usdcDeployment.abi, usdcDeployment.address);\n19\t\n20\t  const [deployer, alice] = await ethers.getSigners();\n21\t  // deployer is seeded with 1,000,000 USDC by the deploy script. Give alice some.\n22\t  await token.transfer(alice.address, usdc(\"1000\"));\n23\t\n24\t  return { staker, token, deployer, alice };\n25\t}\n26\t\n27\tdescribe(\"Staker\", function () {\n28\t  it(\"Uses a 6-decimal USDC token\", async function () {\n29\t    const { token } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n30\t    expect(await token.decimals()).to.equal(6);\n31\t    expect(await token.symbol()).to.equal(\"USDC\");\n32\t  });\n33\t\n34\t  it(\"Wires the Staker to the USDC address\", async function () {\n35\t    const { staker, token } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n36\t    expect(await staker.usdc()).to.equal(await token.getAddress());\n37\t  });\n38\t\n39\t  it(\"Stakes USDC after approval and tracks the position\", async function () {\n40\t    const { staker, token, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n41\t    const stakerAddress = await staker.getAddress();\n42\t\n43\t    await token.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddress, usdc(\"250\"));\n44\t    await expect(staker.connect(alice).stake(usdc(\"250\")))\n45\t      .to.emit(staker, \"Staked\")\n46\t staker.totalStaked()).to.equal(usdc(\"250\"));\n50\t    expect(await token.balanceOf(stakerAddress)).to.equal(usdc(\"250\"));\n51\t    expect(await token.balanceOf(alice.address)).to.equal(usdc(\"750\"));\n52\t  });\n53\t\n54\t  it(\"Withdraws the full position\", async function () {\n55\t    const { staker, token, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n56\t    const stakerAddress = await staker.getAddress();\n57\t\n58\t    await token.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddress, usdc(\"250\"));\n59\t    await staker.connect(alice).stake(usdc(\"250\"));\n60\t\n61\t    await expect(staker.connect(alice).withdraw())\n62\t      .to.emit(staker, \"Withdrawn\")\n63\t      .withArgs(alice.address, usdc(\"250\"));\n64\t\n65\t    expect(await staker.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(0n);\n66\t    expect(await staker.totalStaked()).to.equal(0n);\n67\t    expect(await token.balanceOf(alice.address)).to.equal(usdc(\"1000\"));\n68\t  });\n69\t\n70\t  it(\"Accumulates across multiple stakes\", async function () {\n71\t    const { staker, token, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n72\t    const stakerAddress = await staker.getAddress();\n73\t\n74\t    await token.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddress, usdc(\"300\"));\n75\t    await staker.connect(alice).stake(usdc(\"100\"));\n76\t    await staker.connect(alice).stake(usdc(\"200\"));\n77\t\n78\t    expect(await staker.stakedBalance(alice.address)).to.equal(usdc(\"300\"));\n79\t  });\n80\t\n81\t  it(\"Reverts when staking zero\", async function () {\n82\t    const { staker, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n83\t    await expect(staker.connect(alice).stake(0n)).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(staker, \"ZeroAmount\");\n84\t  });\n85\t\n86\t  it(\"Reverts when withdrawing with no position\", async function () {\n87\t    const { staker, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n88\t    await expect(staker.connect(alice).withdraw()).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(staker, \"NothingStaked\");\n89\t  });\n90\t\n91\t  it(\"Keeps user positions independent\", async function () {\n92\t    const { staker, token, deployer, alice } = await networkHelpers.loadFixture(deployFixture);\n93\t    const stakerAddress = await staker.getAddress();\n94\t\n95\t    await token.connect(alice).approve(stakerAddress, usdc(\"400\"));\n96\t    await staker.connect(alice).stake(usdc(\"400\"));\n97\t\n98\t    await token.connect(deployer).approve(stakerAddress, usdc(\"100\"));\n99\t    await staker.connect(deployer).stake(usdc(\"100\"));\n100\t\n101\t    expect(await {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0151XA5auWMaEventTj1aEaq","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"use client\";\n2\t\n3\timport { useState } from \"react\";\n4\timport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\n5\timport { useConnectModal } from \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit\";\n6\timport { Address, Balance } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\n7\timport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\n8\timport { useAccount, useSwitchChain } from \"wagmi\";\n9\timport { ArrowDownTrayIcon, BanknotesIcon, WalletIcon } from \"@heroicons/react/24/outline\";\n10\timport {\n11\t  useDeployedContractInfo,\n12\t  useScaffoldReadContract,\n13\t  useScaffoldWriteContract,\n14\t  useTargetNetwork,\n15\t} from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\n16\timport { getParsedError, notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n17\t\n18\tconst usd = new Intl.NumberFormat(\"en-US\", { style: \"currency\", currency: \"USD\" });\n19\t\n20\t// USDC is a dollar stablecoin, so token units map ~1:1 to USD.\n21\tconst formatToken = (value: bigint | undefined, decimals: number) =>\n22\t  value === undefined\n23\t    ? \"—\"\n24\t    : Number(formatUnits(value, decimals)).toLocaleString(\"en-US\", {\n25\t        minimumFractionDigits: 2,\n26\t        maximumFractionDigits: 2,\n27\t      });\n28\t\n29\tconst formatUsd = (value: bigint | undefined, decimals: number) =>\n30\t  value === undefined ? \"\" : usd.format(Number(formatUnits(value, decimals)));\n31\t\n32\tconst Stake: NextPage = () => {\n33\t  const { address, chain } = useAccount();\n34\t  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n35\t  const { openConnectModal } = useConnectModal();\n36\t  const { switchChain, isPending: isSwitching } = useSwitchChain();\n37\t\n38\t  const { data: stakerContract } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"Staker\" });\n39\t  const stakerAddress = stakerContract?.address;\n40\t\n41\t  const { data: usdcDecimals } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"USDC\", functionName: \"decimals\" });\n42\t  const decimals = usdcDecimals ?? 6;\n43\t\n44\t  const { data: walletBalance, refetch: refetchWallet } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n45\t    contractName: \"USDC\",\n46\t    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n47\t    args: [address],\n48\t  });\n49\t  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n50\t    contractName: \"USDC\",\n51\t    functionName: \"allowance\",\n52\t    args: [address, stakerAddress],\n53\t  });\n54\t  const { data: staked, refetch: refetchStaked } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n55\t    contractName: \"Staker\",\n56\t    functionName: \"stakedBalance\",\n57\t    args: [address],\n58\t  });\n59\t\n60\t  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n61\t  const { writeContractAsync: writeStaker } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"Staker\" });\n62\t\n63\t  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n64\t  const [isApproving, setIsApproving] = useState(false);\n65\t  const [approveCooldown, setApproveCooldown] = useState(false);\n66\t  const [isStaking, setIsStaking] = useState(false);\n67\t  const [isWithdrawing, setIsWithdrawing] = useState(false);\n68\t  const [txError, setTxError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n69\t\n70\t  // Parse the input safely — parseUnits throws on malformed values.\n71\t  let parsedAmount: bigint | null = null;\n72\t  try {\n73\t    parsedAmount = amount ? parseUnits(amount, decimals) : null;\n74\t  } catch {\n75\t    parsedAmount = null;\n76\t  }\n77\t\n78\t  const hasAmount = parsedAmount !== null && parsedAmount > 0n;\n79\t  const exceedsBalance = hasAmount && walletBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount! > walletBalance;\n80\t  const needsApproval = hasAmount && !exceedsBalance && allowance !== undefined && allowance < parsedAmount!;\n81\t  const wrongNetwork = !!chain && chain.id !== targetNetwork.id;\n82\t\n83\t  const handleMax = () => {\n84\t    if (walletBalance !== undefined) setAmount(formatUnits(walletBalance, decimals));\n85\t  };\n86\t\n87\t  const handleApprove = async () => {\n88\t    if (!parsedAmount || !stakerAddress) return;\n89\t    setTxError(null);\n90\t    try {\n91\t      setIsApproving(true);\n92\t      await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [stakerAddress, parsedAmount] });\n93\t      // Cover the confirmation → RPC-cache gap: keep the button locked while the\n94\t      // fresh allowance propagates so the user can't double-submit.\n95\t      setApproveCooldown(true);\n96\t      await refetchAllowance();\n97\t      setTimeout(() => setApproveCooldown(false), 4000);\n98\t    } catch (e) {\n99\t      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n100\t    } finally {\n101\t      setIsApproving(false);\n102\t    }\n103\t  };\n104\t\n105\t  const handleStake = async () => {\n106\t    if (!parsedAmount) return;\n107\t    setTxError(null);\n108\t    try {\n109\t      setIsStaking(true);\n110\t      await writeStaker({ functionName: \"stake\", args: [parsedAmount] });\n111\t      notification.success(\"Staked successfully\");\n112\t      setAmount(\"\");\n113\t      await Promise.all([refetchWallet(), refetchStaked(), refetchAllowance()]);\n114\t    } catch (e) {\n115\t      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n116\t    } finally {\n117\t      setIsStaking(false);\n118\t    }\n119\t  };\n120\t\n121\t  const handleWithdraw = async () => {\n122\t    setTxError(null);\n123\t    try {\n124\t      setIsWithdrawing(true);\n125\t      await writeStaker({ functionName: \"withdraw\" });\n126\t      notification.success(\"Withdrew your full position\");\n127\t      await Promise.all([refetchWallet(), refetchStaked()]);\n128\t    } catch (e) {\n129\t      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n130\t    } finally {\n131\t      setIsWithdrawing(false);\n132\t    }\n133\t  };\n134\t\n135\t  const renderPrimaryAction = () => {\n136\t    if (!address) {\n137\t      return (\n138\t        <button className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\" onClick={openConnectModal} type=\"button\">\n139\t          Connect Wallet\n140\t        </button>\n141\t      );\n142\t    }\n143\t    if (wrongNetwork) {\n144\t      return (\n145\t        <button\n146\t          className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\"\n147\t          disabled={isSwitching}\n148\t          onClick={() => switchChain({ chainId: targetNetwork.id })}\n149\t          type=\"button\"\n150\t        >\n151\t          {isSwitching ? (\n152\t            <>\n153\t              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Switching…\n154\t            </>\n155\t          ) : (\n156\t            `Switch to ${targetNetwork.name}`\n157\t          )}\n158\t        </button>\n159\t      );\n160\t    }\n161\t    if (!stakerAddress) {\n162\t      return (\n163\t        <button className=\"btn w-full\" disabled type=\"button\">\n164\t          Staking not available on {chain?.name ?? \"this network\"}\n165\t        </button>\n166\t      );\n167\t    }\n168\t    if (needsApproval) {\n169\t      const busy = isApproving || approveCooldown;\n170\t      return (\n171\t        <button className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\" disabled={busy} onClick={handleApprove} type=\"button\">\n172\t          {isApproving ? (\n173\t            <>\n174\t              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Approving…\n175\t            </>\n176\t          ) : approveCooldown ? (\n177\t            <>\n178\t              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Confirming…\n179\t            </>\n180\t          ) : (\n181\t            \"Approve USDC\"\n182\t          )}\n183\t        </button>\n184\t      );\n185\t    }\n186\t    return (\n187\t      <button\n188\t        className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\"\n189\t        disabled={!hasAmount || exceedsBalance || isStaking}\n190\t        onClick={handleStake}\n191\t        type=\"button\"\n192\t      >\n193\t        {isStaking ? (\n194\t          <>\n195\t            <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Staking…\n196\t          </>\n197\t        ) : (\n198\t          \"Stake\"\n199\t        )}\n200\t      </button>\n201\t    );\n202\t  };\n203\t\n204\t  return (\n205\t    <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center grow w-full px-4 py-10\">\n206\t      <div className=\"w-full max-w-lg\">\n207\t        <h1 className=\"text-center text-4xl font-bold mb-1\">Stake USDC</h1>\n208\t        <p className=\"text-center text-base-content/70 mb-8\">\n209\t          Deposit USDC and withdraw your full position anytime. No lockups.\n210\t        </p>\n211\t\n212\t        {/* Account balances */}\n213\t        <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-md mb-6\">\n214\t          <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n215\t            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n216\t              <span className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium\">\n217\t                <WalletIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" /> Wallet USDC\n218\t              </span>\n219\t              <span className=\"text-right\">\n220\t                <span className=\"font-semibold\">{formatToken(walletBalance, decimals)} USDC</span>\n221\t                <span className=\"block text-xs text-base-content/60\">~{formatUsd(walletBalance, decimals)}</span>\n222\t              </span>\n223\t            </div>\n224\t\n225\t            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n226\t              <span className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium\">\n227\t                <BanknotesIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" /> ETH{\" \"}\n228\t                <span className=\"text-xs text-base-content/60\">(for gas)</span>\n229\t              </span>\n230\t              {address ? <Balance address={address} chain={targetNetwork} /> : <span>—</span>}\n231\t            </div>\n232\t\n233\t            <div className=\"divider my-0\" />\n234\t\n235\t            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n236\t              <span className=\"text-sm font-medium\">Your staked position</span>\n237\t              <span className=\"text-right\">\n238\t                <span className=\"font-semibold\">{formatToken(staked, decimals)} USDC</span>\n239\t                <span className=\"block text-xs text-base-content/60\">~{formatUsd(staked, decimals)}</span>\n240\t              </span>\n241\t            </div>\n242\t          </div>\n243\t        </div>\n244\t\n245\t        {/* Stake */}\n246\t        <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-md mb-6\">\n247\t          <div className=\"card-body gap-3\">\n248\t            <label className=\"text-sm font-medium\" htmlFor=\"stake-amount\">\n249\t              Amount to stake\n250\t            </label>\n251\t            <div className=\"join w-full\">\n252\t              <input\n253\t                id=\"stake-amount\"\n254\t                className={`input input-bordered join-item w-full ${exceedsBalance ? \"input-error\" : \"\"}`}\n255\t                placeholder=\"0.00\"\n256\t                inputMode=\"decimal\"\n257\t                value={amount}\n258\t                onChange={e => {\n259\t                  // Allow only numbers with an optional single decimal point.\n260\t                  const next = e.target.value;\n261\t                  if (next === \"\" || /^\\d*\\.?\\d*$/.test(next)) setAmount(next);\n262\t                }}\n263\t              />\n264\t              <button className=\"btn btn-ghost join-item\" onClick={handleMax} disabled={!walletBalance} type=\"button\">\n265\t                MAX\n266\t              </button>\n267\t            </div>\n268\t            <div className=\"flex justify-between text-xs text-base-content/60 min-h-4\">\n269\t              <span>{hasAmount ? (\n297\t                <>\n298\t                  <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Withdrawing…\n299\t                </>\n300\t              ) : (\n301\t                <>\n302\t                  <ArrowDownTrayIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" /> Withdraw all\n303\t                </>\n304\t              )}\n305\t            </button>\n306\t          </div>\n307\t        </div>\n308\t\n309\t        {stakerAddress && (\n310\t          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-6 text-xs text-base-content/50\">\n311\t            <span>Staking contract:</span>\n312\t            <Address address={stakerAddress} size=\"xs\" chain={targetNetwork} />\n313\t          </div>\n314\t        )}\n315\t      </div>\n316\t    </div>\n317\t  );\n318\t};\n319\t\n320\texport default Stake;\n321\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_016HW7cpP9otX9FZSLdnUMc9","session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"fdd700b9-61ce-4eb2-91e5-d6ddb3587a39","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:09:39.896Z","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review staking dApp code"}

walletConnectProjectId: string;\n11\t  burnerWalletMode: \"localNetworksOnly\" | \"allNetworks\" | \"disabled\";\n12\t};\n13\t\n14\texport type ScaffoldConfig = BaseConfig ;\n15\t\n16\texport const alchemyApiKey: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY || DEFAULT_ALCHEMY_API_KEY,\n32\t  // If you want to use a different RPC for a specific network, you can add it here.\n33\t  // The key is the chain ID, and the value is the HTTP RPC URL\n34\t  rpcOverrides: {\n35\t    // Example:\n36\t    // [chains.mainnet.id]: \"https://mainnet.rpc.buidlguidl.com\",\n37\t  },\n38\t  // This is ours and in the Vercel/system env config for live apps.\n42\t  walletConnectProjectId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLET_CONNECT_PROJECT_ID || '3a8170812b534d0ff9d794f19a901d64',\n43\t  // Configure Burner Wallet visibility:\n44\t  // - \"localNetworksOnly\": only show when all target networks are local (hardhat/anvil)\n45\t  // - \"allNetworks\": show on any configured target networks\n46\t  // - {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLYVnsfZhxZgB9EvYexP","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"All OpenZeppelin v5.6.1 paths are correct, and overriding `view` `decimals()` with `pure` is valid Solidity. Let me wait for the code review to come back before finalizing.\n\nThe core build is complete. Here's what I've implemented while the review runs:\n\n**Contracts** (`packages/hardhat/contracts/`)\n- `Staker.sol` — minimal USDC vault: `stake(amount)` / `withdraw()` (full position), `SafeERC20` + `ReentrancyGuard`, CEI ordering, custom errors, `Staked`/`Withdrawn` events. No rewards.\n- `MockUSDC.sol` — faithful local stand-in: 6 decimals, \"USD Coin\"/\"USDC\", open `mint` for funding test accounts.\n\n**Deploy** (`00_deploy_staker.ts`) — network-aware: uses real USDC on Base (`0x8335…2913`) / Base Sepolia, deploys+seeds the mock on local, then wires the Staker.\n\n**Tests** (`test/Staker.ts`) — stake/withdraw, accounting, per-user isolation, zero/empty reverts.\n\n**Frontend**\n- `/stake` page: four-state action flow (Connect → Switch Network → Approve → Stake), per-action pending states, approve submitting+cooldown to close the double-submit window, wallet USDC / ETH-for-gas / staked position all with USD context, MAX button, inline error translation via `getParsedError`, semantic theme tokens.\n- `externalContracts.ts` — real USDC registered for Base + Base Sepolia.\n- `scaffold.config.ts` targets Base (+ hardhat for local), nav link, home page, and metadata updated.\n\nOne deliberate note: `deployedContracts.ts` stays the empty auto-generated stub — `yarn deploy` regenerates it with the Staker (standard SE-2 flow), which is required before the frontend type-resolves `\"Staker\"` and runs."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":746,"cache_read_input_tokens":117070,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":746},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"210cc89d-eb6f-4326-a9ff-3ccbe93ae8ed","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:11:03.726Z","request_id":"req_011CdLYVmT5xCdZPEKqeTYDs"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLYatEmN5appBxnWc2WY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Contracts and core logic are solid. Findings below, ordered by importance. I did not modify any files.\n\n## Correctness / build\n\n**1. `chain` prop passed to `Balance` and `Address` — likely a TS build break**\n`packages/nextjs/app/stake/page.tsx:230` (`<Balance address={address} chain={targetNetwork} />`) and `:312` (`<Address ... chain={targetNetwork} />`). No other usage in this repo passes `chain` to these components (see `RainbowKitCustomConnectButton/index.tsx:48`, `Faucet.tsx:106`, `AddressComponent.tsx:30`), and the SE-2/@scaffold-ui `Balance` component derives its chain from `useTargetNetwork` internally rather than accepting a `chain` prop. If `BalanceProps` doesn't include `chain`, `yarn next:build` fails under strict TS. Verify against the installed `@scaffold-ui/components@0.1.12` typings; if unsupported, drop the prop.\n\n**2. Base Sepolia is half-wired**\n`deploy/00_deploy_staker.ts:9` and `externalContracts.ts:102-107` fully support chainId 84532, but `scaffold.config.ts:21-24` `targetNetworks` only lists `base` + `hardhat`. Net effect: the testnet config is dead code — the frontend can never actually run against Base Sepolia. Either add `chains.baseSepolia` to `targetNetworks` or drop the 84532 entries.\n\n**3. Burner wallet silently disabled for local dev**\n`scaffold.config.ts:47` `burnerWalletMode: 'localNetworksOnly'` combined with `base` in `targetNetworks` (`:22`) means the burner never shows — `localNetworksOnly` requires *all* target networks to be local. Local hardhat development against the MockUSDC therefore loses the burner wallet. If local dev with burner is intended, this needs `\"allNetworks\"` or a local-only target set.\n\n## SE-2 conventions\n\n**4. Raw `<input>` instead of the mandated amount-input component**\n`page.tsx:252-263` uses a hand-rolled `<input>` + regex (`:261`) for a token amount. AGENTS.md requires `@scaffold-ui/components` inputs (`IntegerInput`/`EtherInput`) for web3 amount fields, which already handle decimal validation and unit conversion.\n\n**5. Input allows more precision than USDC supports**\nThe regex at `page.tsx:261` accepts arbitrary decimal places. `parseUnits(amount, 6)` at `:73` will silently round anything beyond 6 dp (viem rounds rather than throws), so the staked amount can differ from what the user typed with no feedback. The `IntegerInput` component (finding 4) avoids this.\n\n## Minor / cosmetic\n\n- `page.tsx:95-97`: `approveCooldown` is a hard-coded 4s `setTimeout` (magic number, longer than the 3s `pollingInterval`). The read hook already re-polls allowance, so this is a UX crutch — acceptable but hacky, and it never force-refetches after the timeout.\n- `scaffold.config.ts:16,28,38`: shared default Alchemy key / WalletConnect id and typo comments (\"This is ours ...\") — cosmetic, but ship real keys via env for a production Base target.\n- `scaffold.config.ts:26`: `pollingInterval: 3000` for Base (L2) — only marginally below the 4000 default; fine, just noting AGENTS.md's L2 guidance.\n\n## What's correct (no action)\n\n- `Staker.sol`: checks-effects-interactions ordering (`:41-44`, `:54-57`), `nonReentrant`, `SafeERC20`, immutable `usdc`, zero-address guard, safe `totalStaked -=` (can't underflow). No admin/rescue is an intentional trustless design; note that USDC accidentally sent directly (not via `stake`) is unrecoverable — acceptable for a minimal vault.\n- `MockUSDC.sol`: correct 6-decimal override, open `mint` clearly documented as local-only.\n- Deploy script correctly branches real-vs-mock USDC and registers the mock as `\"USDC\"` for the frontend.\n- `test/Staker.ts`: covers stake/withdraw/accumulate/independence/zero/nothing-staked. Reasonable coverage. One gap: no reentrancy or non-approval-revert test, but not required.\n\nRelevant files:\n- `/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T075643Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/packages/nextjs/app/stake/page.tsx`\n- `/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T075643Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/packages/nextjs/scaffold.config.ts`\n- `/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T075643Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/packages/nextjs/contracts/externalContracts.ts`\n- `/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-001/2026-07-24T075643Z-claude-with-skill-1/workspace/packages/hardhat/deploy/00_deploy_staker.ts`"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2316,"cache_read_input_tokens":31473,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2316,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_016HW7cpP9otX9FZSLdnUMc9","session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"e26f1498-c8b0-4f67-9893-d627ada2705a","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:12:30.282Z","request_id":"req_011CdLYaoAAj6E2K2smqHMHH","subagent_type":"grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer","task_description":"Review staking dApp code"}

{ Address, Balance } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";","newString":"import { Address, Balance, BaseInput } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";","originalFile":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useState } from \"react\";\nimport type { NextPage } from \"next\";\nimport { useConnectModal } from \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit\";\nimport { Address, Balance } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";\nimport { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";\nimport { useAccount, useSwitchChain } from \"wagmi\";\nimport { ArrowDownTrayIcon, BanknotesIcon, WalletIcon } from \"@heroicons/react/24/outline\";\nimport {\n  useDeployedContractInfo,\n  useScaffoldReadContract,\n  useScaffoldWriteContract,\n  useTargetNetwork,\n} from \"~~/hooks/scaffold-eth\";\nimport { getParsedError, notification } from \"~~/utils/scaffold-eth\";\n\nconst usd = new Intl.NumberFormat(\"en-US\", { style: \"currency\", currency: \"USD\" });\n\n// USDC is a dollar stablecoin, so token units map ~1:1 to USD.\nconst formatToken = (value: bigint | undefined, decimals: number) =>\n  value === undefined\n    ? \"—\"\n    : Number(formatUnits(value, decimals)).toLocaleString(\"en-US\", {\n        minimumFractionDigits: 2,\n        maximumFractionDigits: 2,\n      });\n\nconst formatUsd = (value: bigint | undefined, decimals: number) =>\n  value === undefined ? \"\" : usd.format(Number(formatUnits(value, decimals)));\n\nconst Stake: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address, chain } = useAccount();\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const { openConnectModal } = useConnectModal();\n  const { switchChain, isPending: isSwitching } = useSwitchChain();\n\n  const { data: stakerContract } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"Staker\" });\n  const stakerAddress = stakerContract?.address;\n\n  const { data: usdcDecimals } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"USDC\", functionName: \"decimals\" });\n  const decimals = usdcDecimals ?? 6;\n\n  const { data: walletBalance, refetch: refetchWallet } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [address],\n  });\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [address, stakerAddress],\n  });\n  const { data: staked, refetch: refetchStaked } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"Staker\",\n    functionName: \"stakedBalance\",\n    args: [address],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeStaker } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"Staker\" });\n\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [isApproving, setIsApproving] = useState(false);\n  const [approveCooldown, setApproveCooldown] = useState(false);\n  const [isStaking, setIsStaking] = useState(false);\n  const [isWithdrawing, setIsWithdrawing] = useState(false);\n  const [txError, setTxError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  // Parse the input safely — parseUnits throws on malformed values.\n  let parsedAmount: bigint | null = null;\n  try {\n    parsedAmount = amount ? parseUnits(amount, decimals) : null;\n  } catch {\n    parsedAmount = null;\n  }\n\n  const hasAmount = parsedAmount !== null && parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const exceedsBalance = hasAmount && walletBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount! > walletBalance;\n  const needsApproval = hasAmount && !exceedsBalance && allowance !== undefined && allowance < parsedAmount!;\n  const wrongNetwork = !!chain && chain.id !== targetNetwork.id;\n\n  const handleMax = () => {\n    if (walletBalance !== undefined) setAmount(formatUnits(walletBalance, decimals));\n  };\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!parsedAmount || !stakerAddress) return;\n    setTxError(null);\n    try {\n      setIsApproving(true);\n      await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [stakerAddress, parsedAmount] });\n      // Cover the confirmation → RPC-cache gap: keep the button locked while the\n      // fresh allowance propagates so the user can't double-submit.\n      setApproveCooldown(true);\n      await refetchAllowance();\n      setTimeout(() => setApproveCooldown(false), 4000);\n    } catch (e) {\n      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setIsApproving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleStake = async () => {\n    if (!parsedAmount) return;\n    setTxError(null);\n    try {\n      setIsStaking(true);\n      await writeStaker({ functionName: \"stake\", args: [parsedAmount] });\n      notification.success(\"Staked successfully\");\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      await Promise.all([refetchWallet(), refetchStaked(), refetchAllowance()]);\n    } catch (e) {\n      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setIsStaking(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleWithdraw = async () => {\n    setTxError(null);\n    try {\n      setIsWithdrawing(true);\n      await writeStaker({ functionName: \"withdraw\" });\n      notification.success(\"Withdrew your full position\");\n      await Promise.all([refetchWallet(), refetchStaked()]);\n    } catch (e) {\n      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setIsWithdrawing(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const renderPrimaryAction = () => {\n    if (!address) {\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\" onClick={openConnectModal} type=\"button\">\n          Connect Wallet\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    if (wrongNetwork) {\n      return (\n        <button\n          className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\"\n          disabled={isSwitching}\n          onClick={() => switchChain({ chainId: targetNetwork.id })}\n          type=\"button\"\n        >\n          {isSwitching ? (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Switching…\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            `Switch to ${targetNetwork.name}`\n          )}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    if (!stakerAddress) {\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn w-full\" disabled type=\"button\">\n          Staking not available on {chain?.name ?? \"this network\"}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    if (needsApproval) {\n      const busy = isApproving || approveCooldown;\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\" disabled={busy} onClick={handleApprove} type=\"button\">\n          {isApproving ? (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Approving…\n            </>\n          ) : approveCooldown ? (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Confirming…\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            \"Approve USDC\"\n          )}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    return (\n      <button\n        className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\"\n        disabled={!hasAmount || exceedsBalance || isStaking}\n        onClick={handleStake}\n        type=\"button\"\n      >\n        {isStaking ? (\n          <>\n            <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Staking…\n          </>\n        ) : (\n          \"Stake\"\n        )}\n      </button>\n    );\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center grow w-full px-4 py-10\">\n      <div className=\"w-full max-w-lg\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-center text-4xl font-bold mb-1\">Stake USDC</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-center text-base-content/70 mb-8\">\n          Deposit USDC and withdraw your full position anytime. No lockups.\n        </p>\n\n        {/* Account balances */}\n        <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-md mb-6\">\n          <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n              <span className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium\">\n                <WalletIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" /> Wallet USDC\n              </span>\n              <span className=\"text-right\">\n                <span className=\"font-semibold\">{formatToken(walletBalance, decimals)} USDC</span>\n                <span className=\"block text-xs text-base-content/60\">~{formatUsd(walletBalance, decimals)}</span>\n              </span>\n            </div>\n\n            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n              <span className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium\">\n                <BanknotesIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" /> ETH{\" \"}\n                <span className=\"text-xs text-base-content/60\">(for gas)</span>\n              </span>\n              {address ? <Balance address={address} chain={targetNetwork} /> : <span>—</span>}\n            </div>\n\n            <div className=\"divider my-0\" />\n\n            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n              <span className=\"text-sm font-medium\">Your staked position</span>\n              <span className=\"text-right\">\n                <span className=\"font-semibold\">{formatToken(staked, decimals)} USDC</span>\n                <span className=\"block text-xs text-base-content/60\">~{formatUsd(staked, decimals)}</span>\n              </span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Stake */}\n        <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-md mb-6\">\n          <div className=\"card-body gap-3\">\n            <label className=\"text-sm font-medium\" htmlFor=\"stake-amount\">\n              Amount to stake\n            </label>\n            <div className=\"join w-full\">\n              <input\n                id=\"stake-amount\"\n                className={`input input-bordered join-item w-full ${exceedsBalance ? \"input-error\" : \"\"}`}\n                placeholder=\"0.00\"\n                inputMode=\"decimal\"\n                value={amount}\n                onChange={e => {\n                  // Allow only numbers with an optional single decimal point.\n                  const next = e.target.value;\n                  if (next === \"\" || /^\\d*\\.?\\d*$/.test(next)) setAmount(next);\n                }}\n              />\n              <button className=\"btn btn-ghost join-item\" onClick={handleMax} disabled={!walletBalance} type=\"button\">\n                MAX\n              </button>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"flex justify-between text-xs text-base-content/60 min-h-4\">\n              <span>{hasAmount ? `~${usd.format(Number(amount))}` : \"\"}</span>\n              {exceedsBalance && <span className=\"text-error\">Exceeds wallet balance</span>}\n            </div>\n\n            {renderPrimaryAction()}\n\n            {txError && (\n              <div className=\"alert alert-error text-sm py-2\">\n                <span className=\"break-words\">{txError}</span>\n              </div>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Withdraw */}\n        <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-md\">\n          <div className=\"card-body gap-3\">\n            <span className=\"text-sm font-medium\">Withdraw</span>\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-base-content/60\">\n              Withdraws your entire staked position ({formatToken(staked, decimals)} USDC) back to your wallet.\n            </p>\n            <button\n              className=\"btn btn-outline btn-primary w-full\"\n              disabled={!address || wrongNetwork || !staked || staked === 0n || isWithdrawing}\n              onClick={handleWithdraw}\n              type=\"button\"\n            >\n              {isWithdrawing ? (\n                <>\n                  <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Withdrawing…\n                </>\n              ) : (\n                <>\n                  <ArrowDownTrayIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" /> Withdraw all\n                </>\n              )}\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {stakerAddress && (\n          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-6 text-xs text-base-content/50\">\n            <span>Staking contract:</span>\n            <Address address={stakerAddress} size=\"xs\" chain={targetNetwork} />\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default Stake;\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":3,"oldLines":7,"newStart":3,"newLines":7,"lines":[" import { useState } from \"react\";"," import type { NextPage } from \"next\";"," import { useConnectModal } from \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit\";","-import { Address, Balance } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";","+import { Address, Balance, BaseInput } from \"@scaffold-ui/components\";"," import { formatUnits, parseUnits } from \"viem\";"," import { useAccount, useSwitchChain } from \"wagmi\";"," import { ArrowDownTrayIcon, BanknotesIcon, WalletIcon } from \"@heroicons/react/24/outline\";"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

after confirmation while the fresh\n// allowance propagates through the RPC cache, so the user can't double-submit.\nconst APPROVE_COOLDOWN_MS = 4000;\n\n// USDC is a dollar stablecoin, so token units map ~1:1 to USD.\nconst formatToken = (value: bigint | undefined, decimals: number) =>\n  value === undefined\n    ? \"—\"\n    : Number(formatUnits(value, decimals)).toLocaleString(\"en-US\", {\n        minimumFractionDigits: 2,\n        maximumFractionDigits: 2,\n      });\n\nconst formatUsd = (value: bigint | undefined, decimals: number) =>\n  value === undefined ? \"\" : usd.format(Number(formatUnits(value, decimals)));\n\nconst Stake: NextPage = () => {\n  const { address, chain } = useAccount();\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const { openConnectModal } = useConnectModal();\n  const { switchChain, isPending: isSwitching } = useSwitchChain();\n\n  const { data: stakerContract } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"Staker\" });\n  const stakerAddress = stakerContract?.address;\n\n  const { data: usdcDecimals } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"USDC\", functionName: \"decimals\" });\n  const decimals = usdcDecimals ?? 6;\n\n  const { data: walletBalance, refetch: refetchWallet } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [address],\n  });\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [address, stakerAddress],\n  });\n  const { data: staked, refetch: refetchStaked } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"Staker\",\n    functionName: \"stakedBalance\",\n    args: [address],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeStaker } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"Staker\" });\n\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [isApproving, setIsApproving] = useState(false);\n  const [approveCooldown, setApproveCooldown] = useState(false);\n  const [isStaking, setIsStaking] = useState(false);\n  const [isWithdrawing, setIsWithdrawing] = useState(false);\n  const [txError, setTxError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  // Parse the input safely — parseUnits throws on malformed values.\n  let parsedAmount: bigint | null = null;\n  try {\n    parsedAmount = amount ? parseUnits(amount, decimals) : null;\n  } catch {\n    parsedAmount = null;\n  }\n\n  const hasAmount = parsedAmount !== null && parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const exceedsBalance = hasAmount && walletBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount! > walletBalance;\n  const needsApproval = hasAmount && !exceedsBalance && allowance !== undefined && allowance < parsedAmount!;\n  const wrongNetwork = !!chain && chain.id !== targetNetwork.id;\n\n  const handleMax = () => {\n    if (walletBalance !== undefined) setAmount(formatUnits(walletBalance, decimals));\n  };\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!parsedAmount || !stakerAddress) return;\n    setTxError(null);\n    try {\n      setIsApproving(true);\n      await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [stakerAddress, parsedAmount] });\n      // Cover the confirmation → RPC-cache gap: keep the button locked while the\n      // fresh allowance propagates so the user can't double-submit.\n      setApproveCooldown(true);\n      await refetchAllowance();\n      setTimeout(() => setApproveCooldown(false), APPROVE_COOLDOWN_MS);\n    } catch (e) {\n      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setIsApproving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleStake = async () => {\n    if (!parsedAmount) return;\n    setTxError(null);\n    try {\n      setIsStaking(true);\n      await writeStaker({ functionName: \"stake\", args: [parsedAmount] });\n      notification.success(\"Staked successfully\");\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      await Promise.all([refetchWallet(), refetchStaked(), refetchAllowance()]);\n    } catch (e) {\n      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setIsStaking(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleWithdraw = async () => {\n    setTxError(null);\n    try {\n      setIsWithdrawing(true);\n      await writeStaker({ functionName: \"withdraw\" });\n      notification.success(\"Withdrew your full position\");\n      await Promise.all([refetchWallet(), refetchStaked()]);\n    } catch (e) {\n      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setIsWithdrawing(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const renderPrimaryAction = () => {\n    if (!address) {\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\" onClick={openConnectModal} type=\"button\">\n          Connect Wallet\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    if (wrongNetwork) {\n      return (\n        <button\n          className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\"\n          disabled={isSwitching}\n          onClick={() => switchChain({ chainId: targetNetwork.id })}\n          type=\"button\"\n        >\n          {isSwitching ? (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Switching…\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            `Switch to ${targetNetwork.name}`\n          )}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    if (!stakerAddress) {\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn w-full\" disabled type=\"button\">\n          Staking not available on {chain?.name ?? \"this network\"}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    if (needsApproval) {\n      const busy = isApproving || approveCooldown;\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\" disabled={busy} onClick={handleApprove} type=\"button\">\n          {isApproving ? (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Approving…\n            </>\n          ) : approveCooldown ? (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Confirming…\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            \"Approve USDC\"\n          )}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    return (\n      <button\n        className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\"\n        disabled={!hasAmount || exceedsBalance || isStaking}\n        onClick={handleStake}\n        type=\"button\"\n      >\n        {isStaking ? (\n          <>\n            <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Staking…\n          </>\n        ) : (\n          \"Stake\"\n        )}\n      </button>\n    );\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center grow w-full px-4 py-10\">\n      <div className=\"w-full max-w-lg\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-center text-4xl font-bold mb-1\">Stake USDC</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-center text-base-content/70 mb-8\">\n          Deposit USDC and withdraw your full position anytime. No lockups.\n        </p>\n\n        {/* Account balances */}\n        <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-md mb-6\">\n          <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n              <span className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium\">\n                <WalletIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" /> Wallet USDC\n              </span>\n              <span className=\"text-right\">\n                <span className=\"font-semibold\">{formatToken(walletBalance, decimals)} USDC</span>\n                <span className=\"block text-xs text-base-content/60\">~{formatUsd(walletBalance, decimals)}</span>\n              </span>\n            </div>\n\n            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n              <span className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium\">\n                <BanknotesIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" /> ETH{\" \"}\n                <span className=\"text-xs text-base-content/60\">(for gas)</span>\n              </span>\n              {address ? <Balance address={address} chain={targetNetwork} /> : <span>—</span>}\n            </div>\n\n            <div className=\"divider my-0\" />\n\n            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n              <span className=\"text-sm font-medium\">Your staked position</span>\n              <span className=\"text-right\">\n                <span className=\"font-semibold\">{formatToken(staked, decimals)} USDC</span>\n                <span className=\"block text-xs text-base-content/60\">~{formatUsd(staked, decimals)}</span>\n              </span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Stake */}\n        <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-md mb-6\">\n          <div className=\"card-body gap-3\">\n            <label className=\"text-sm font-medium\" htmlFor=\"stake-amount\">\n              Amount to stake\n            </label>\n            <div className=\"join w-full\">\n              <input\n                id=\"stake-amount\"\n                className={`input input-bordered join-item w-full ${exceedsBalance ? \"input-error\" : \"\"}`}\n                placeholder=\"0.00\"\n                inputMode=\"decimal\"\n                value={amount}\n                onChange={e => {\n                  // Allow only numbers with an optional single decimal point.\n                  const next = e.target.value;\n                  if (next === \"\" || /^\\d*\\.?\\d*$/.test(next)) setAmount(next);\n                }}\n              />\n              <button className=\"btn btn-ghost join-item\" onClick={handleMax} disabled={!walletBalance} type=\"button\">\n                MAX\n              </button>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"flex justify-between text-xs text-base-content/60 min-h-4\">\n              <span>{hasAmount ? `~${usd.format(Number(amount))}` : \"\"}</span>\n              {exceedsBalance && <span className=\"text-error\">Exceeds wallet balance</span>}\n            </div>\n\n            {renderPrimaryAction()}\n\n            {txError && (\n              <div className=\"alert alert-error text-sm py-2\">\n                <span className=\"break-words\">{txError}</span>\n              </div>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Withdraw */}\n        <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-md\">\n          <div className=\"card-body gap-3\">\n            <span className=\"text-sm font-medium\">Withdraw</span>\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-base-content/60\">\n              Withdraws your entire staked position ({formatToken(staked, decimals)} USDC) back to your wallet.\n            </p>\n            <button\n              className=\"btn btn-outline btn-primary w-full\"\n              disabled={!address || wrongNetwork || !staked || staked === 0n || isWithdrawing}\n              onClick={handleWithdraw}\n              type=\"button\"\n            >\n              {isWithdrawing ? { address, chain } = useAccount();\n  const { targetNetwork } = useTargetNetwork();\n  const { openConnectModal } = useConnectModal();\n  const { switchChain, isPending: isSwitching } = useSwitchChain();\n\n  const { data: stakerContract } = useDeployedContractInfo({ contractName: \"Staker\" });\n  const stakerAddress = stakerContract?.address;\n\n  const { data: usdcDecimals } = useScaffoldReadContract({ contractName: \"USDC\", functionName: \"decimals\" });\n  const decimals = usdcDecimals ?? USDC_DECIMALS;\n\n  const { data: walletBalance, refetch: refetchWallet } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"balanceOf\",\n    args: [address],\n  });\n  const { data: allowance, refetch: refetchAllowance } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"USDC\",\n    functionName: \"allowance\",\n    args: [address, stakerAddress],\n  });\n  const { data: staked, refetch: refetchStaked } = useScaffoldReadContract({\n    contractName: \"Staker\",\n    functionName: \"stakedBalance\",\n    args: [address],\n  });\n\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeUsdc } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"USDC\" });\n  const { writeContractAsync: writeStaker } = useScaffoldWriteContract({ contractName: \"Staker\" });\n\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n  const [isApproving, setIsApproving] = useState(false);\n  const [approveCooldown, setApproveCooldown] = useState(false);\n  const [isStaking, setIsStaking] = useState(false);\n  const [isWithdrawing, setIsWithdrawing] = useState(false);\n  const [txError, setTxError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  // Parse the input safely — parseUnits throws on malformed values.\n  let parsedAmount: bigint | null = null;\n  try {\n    parsedAmount = amount ? parseUnits(amount, decimals) : null;\n  } catch {\n    parsedAmount = null;\n  }\n\n  const hasAmount = parsedAmount !== null && parsedAmount > 0n;\n  const exceedsBalance = hasAmount && walletBalance !== undefined && parsedAmount! > walletBalance;\n  const needsApproval = hasAmount && !exceedsBalance && allowance !== undefined && allowance < parsedAmount!;\n  const wrongNetwork = !!chain && chain.id !== targetNetwork.id;\n\n  const handleMax = () => {\n    if (walletBalance !== undefined) setAmount(formatUnits(walletBalance, decimals));\n  };\n\n  const handleApprove = async () => {\n    if (!parsedAmount || !stakerAddress) return;\n    setTxError(null);\n    try {\n      setIsApproving(true);\n      await writeUsdc({ functionName: \"approve\", args: [stakerAddress, parsedAmount] });\n      // Cover the confirmation → RPC-cache gap: keep the button locked while the\n      // fresh allowance propagates so the user can't double-submit.\n      setApproveCooldown(true);\n      await refetchAllowance();\n      setTimeout(() => setApproveCooldown(false), APPROVE_COOLDOWN_MS);\n    } catch (e) {\n      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setIsApproving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleStake = async () => {\n    if (!parsedAmount) return;\n    setTxError(null);\n    try {\n      setIsStaking(true);\n      await writeStaker({ functionName: \"stake\", args: [parsedAmount] });\n      notification.success(\"Staked successfully\");\n      setAmount(\"\");\n      await Promise.all([refetchWallet(), refetchStaked(), refetchAllowance()]);\n    } catch (e) {\n      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setIsStaking(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleWithdraw = async () => {\n    setTxError(null);\n    try {\n      setIsWithdrawing(true);\n      await writeStaker({ functionName: \"withdraw\" });\n      notification.success(\"Withdrew your full position\");\n      await Promise.all([refetchWallet(), refetchStaked()]);\n    } catch (e) {\n      setTxError(getParsedError(e));\n    } finally {\n      setIsWithdrawing(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const renderPrimaryAction = () => {\n    if (!address) {\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\" onClick={openConnectModal} type=\"button\">\n          Connect Wallet\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    if (wrongNetwork) {\n      return (\n        <button\n          className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\"\n          disabled={isSwitching}\n          onClick={() => switchChain({ chainId: targetNetwork.id })}\n          type=\"button\"\n        >\n          {isSwitching ? (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Switching…\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            `Switch to ${targetNetwork.name}`\n          )}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    if (!stakerAddress) {\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn w-full\" disabled type=\"button\">\n          Staking not available on {chain?.name ?? \"this network\"}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    if (needsApproval) {\n      const busy = isApproving || approveCooldown;\n      return (\n        <button className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\" disabled={busy} onClick={handleApprove} type=\"button\">\n          {isApproving ? (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Approving…\n            </>\n          ) : approveCooldown ? (\n            <>\n              <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Confirming…\n            </>\n          ) : (\n            \"Approve USDC\"\n          )}\n        </button>\n      );\n    }\n    return (\n      <button\n        className=\"btn btn-primary w-full\"\n        disabled={!hasAmount || exceedsBalance || isStaking}\n        onClick={handleStake}\n        type=\"button\"\n      >\n        {isStaking ? (\n          <>\n            <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Staking…\n          </>\n        ) : (\n          \"Stake\"\n        )}\n      </button>\n    );\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center grow w-full px-4 py-10\">\n      <div className=\"w-full max-w-lg\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-center text-4xl font-bold mb-1\">Stake USDC</h1>\n        <p className=\"text-center text-base-content/70 mb-8\">\n          Deposit USDC and withdraw your full position anytime. No lockups.\n        </p>\n\n        {/* Account balances */}\n        <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-md mb-6\">\n          <div className=\"card-body gap-4\">\n            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n              <span className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium\">\n                <WalletIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" /> Wallet USDC\n              </span>\n              <span className=\"text-right\">\n                <span className=\"font-semibold\">{formatToken(walletBalance, decimals)} USDC</span>\n                <span className=\"block text-xs text-base-content/60\">~{formatUsd(walletBalance, decimals)}</span>\n              </span>\n            </div>\n\n            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n              <span className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium\">\n                <BanknotesIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" /> ETH{\" \"}\n                <span className=\"text-xs text-base-content/60\">(for gas)</span>\n              </span>\n              {address ? <Balance address={address} chain={targetNetwork} /> : <span>—</span>}\n            </div>\n\n            <div className=\"divider my-0\" />\n\n            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n              <span className=\"text-sm font-medium\">Your staked position</span>\n              <span className=\"text-right\">\n                <span className=\"font-semibold\">{formatToken(staked, decimals)} USDC</span>\n                <span className=\"block text-xs text-base-content/60\">~{formatUsd(staked, decimals)}</span>\n              </span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Stake */}\n        <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-md mb-6\">\n          <div className=\"card-body gap-3\">\n            <label className=\"text-sm font-medium\" htmlFor=\"stake-amount\">\n              Amount to stake\n            </label>\n            <div className=\"join w-full\">\n              <input\n                id=\"stake-amount\"\n                className={`input input-bordered join-item w-full ${exceedsBalance ? \"input-error\" : \"\"}`}\n                placeholder=\"0.00\"\n                inputMode=\"decimal\"\n                value={amount}\n                onChange={e => {\n                  // Allow only numbers with an optional single decimal point.\n                  const next = e.target.value;\n                  if (next === \"\" || /^\\d*\\.?\\d*$/.test(next)) setAmount(next);\n                }}\n              />\n              <button className=\"btn btn-ghost join-item\" onClick={handleMax} disabled={!walletBalance} type=\"button\">\n                MAX\n              </button>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"flex justify-between text-xs text-base-content/60 min-h-4\">\n              <span>{hasAmount ? `~${usd.format(Number(amount))}` : \"\"}</span>\n              {exceedsBalance && <span className=\"text-error\">Exceeds wallet balance</span>}\n            </div>\n\n            {renderPrimaryAction()}\n\n            {txError && (\n              <div className=\"alert alert-error text-sm py-2\">\n                <span className=\"break-words\">{txError}</span>\n              </div>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Withdraw */}\n        <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-md\">\n          <div className=\"card-body gap-3\">\n            <span className=\"text-sm font-medium\">Withdraw</span>\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-base-content/60\">\n              Withdraws your entire staked position ({formatToken(staked, decimals)} USDC) back to your wallet.\n            </p>\n            <button\n              className=\"btn btn-outline btn-primary w-full\"\n              disabled={!address || wrongNetwork || !staked || staked === 0n || isWithdrawing}\n              onClick={handleWithdraw}\n              type=\"button\"\n            >\n              {isWithdrawing ? (\n                <>\n                  <span className=\"loading loading-spinner loading-sm\" /> Withdrawing…\n                </>\n              ) : (\n                <>\n                  <ArrowDownTrayIcon className=\"h-4 w-4\" /> Withdraw all\n                </>\n              )}\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {stakerAddress && (\n          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-6 text-xs text-base-content/50\">\n            <span>Staking contract:</span>\n            <Address address={stakerAddress} size=\"xs\" chain={targetNetwork} />\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default Stake;\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":253,"oldLines":26,"newStart":253,"newLines":26,"lines":["         {/* Stake */}","         <div className=\"card bg-base-100 shadow-md mb-6\">","           <div className=\"card-body gap-3\">","-            <label className=\"text-sm font-medium\" htmlFor=\"stake-amount\">","-              Amount to stake","-            </label>","-            <div className=\"join w-full\">","-              <input","-                id=\"stake-amount\"","-                className={`input input-bordered join-item w-full ${exceedsBalance ?
