Committed as `d13a144`.\n\n## What I built\n\n**Contracts** (`packages/hardhat/contracts/`)\n- **`Staker.sol`** — minimal USDC vault. `stake(amount)` and `withdraw()` (full position, anytime). `SafeERC20` + `ReentrancyGuard`, checks-effects-interactions ordering, immutable `usdc`, zero-address guard, custom errors, `Staked`/`Withdrawn` events. No rewards, no admin — trustless by design.\n- **`MockUSDC.sol`** — faithful local stand-in: 6 decimals, \"USD Coin\"/\"USDC\" metadata, standard ERC20 semantics, open `mint` (documented local-only).\n- **Deploy** (`00_deploy_staker.ts`) — uses the real USDC on Base (`0x8335…2913`) / Base Sepolia, deploys + seeds the mock on local, then wires the Staker.\n- **Tests** (`test/Staker.ts`) — stake, withdraw, accumulation, per-user isolation, zero/empty reverts.\n\n**Frontend** (`packages/nextjs/`)\n- **`/stake` page** — four-state action flow (Connect → Switch Network → Approve → Stake), separate pending state per action, approve *submitting + cooldown* to close the double-submit window; shows wallet USDC, ETH (for gas), and staked position, each with USD context; MAX button; inline contract-error translation via `getParsedError`; kit `BaseInput` capped at 6 decimals; semantic theme tokens.\n- Targets **Base** (+ Base Sepolia for testing, hardhat for local); real USDC registered in `externalContracts.ts`; nav link, home page, and OG/title metadata updated.\n\n**Review outcome:** ran the repo's `grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer`. Applied its valid fixes (added Base Sepolia to `targetNetworks`, swapped the raw input for the kit's `BaseInput` with a strict 6-decimal cap, named the cooldown constant). Declined three: the `chain` prop is in the `@scaffold-ui` typings (verified), and hiding the burner wallet on a real-users Base app is deliberate.\n\nTwo things to know before it goes live:\n- **Run `yarn deploy --network base`** first — that regenerates `deployedContracts.ts` with the Staker (standard SE-2 flow; the frontend's `\"Staker\"` reference resolves only after it exists there).\n- **Set real `NEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY` / WalletConnect ID** via env for production RPC (the scaffold ships shared defaults).\n\nI did not run the app or deploy, per your instructions — the committed code is the deliverable."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":497,"cache_read_input_tokens":129609,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":497},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d6cd7cf8-a134-48d3-ab34-4c652da4e14e","uuid":"9255b1b1-7f23-402e-b24d-74156ef781eb","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:14:29.287Z","request_id":"req_011CdLYkzYofhebt4Y3QjTwu"}

Committed as `d13a144`.\n\n## What I built\n\n**Contracts** (`packages/hardhat/contracts/`)\n- **`Staker.sol`** — minimal USDC vault. `stake(amount)` and `withdraw()` (full position, anytime). `SafeERC20` + `ReentrancyGuard`, checks-effects-interactions ordering, immutable `usdc`, zero-address guard, custom errors, `Staked`/`Withdrawn` events. No rewards, no admin — trustless by design.\n- **`MockUSDC.sol`** — faithful local stand-in: 6 decimals, \"USD Coin\"/\"USDC\" metadata, standard ERC20 semantics, open `mint` (documented local-only).\n- **Deploy** (`00_deploy_staker.ts`) — uses the real USDC on Base (`0x8335…2913`) / Base Sepolia, deploys + seeds the mock on local, then wires the Staker.\n- **Tests** (`test/Staker.ts`) — stake, withdraw, accumulation, per-user isolation, zero/empty reverts.\n\n**Frontend** (`packages/nextjs/`)\n- **`/stake` page** — four-state action flow (Connect → Switch Network → Approve → Stake), separate pending state per action, approve *submitting + cooldown* to close the double-submit window; shows wallet USDC, ETH (for gas), and staked position, each with USD context; MAX button; inline contract-error translation via `getParsedError`; kit `BaseInput` capped at 6 decimals; semantic theme tokens.\n- Targets **Base** (+ Base Sepolia for testing, hardhat for local); real USDC registered in `externalContracts.ts`; nav link, home page, and OG/title metadata updated.\n\n**Review outcome:** ran the repo's `grumpy-carlos-code-reviewer`. Applied its valid fixes (added Base Sepolia to `targetNetworks`, swapped the raw input for the kit's `BaseInput` with a strict 6-decimal cap, named the cooldown constant). Declined three: the `chain` prop is in the `@scaffold-ui` typings (verified), and hiding the burner wallet on a real-users Base app is deliberate.\n\nTwo things to know before it goes live:\n- **Run `yarn deploy --network base`** first — that regenerates `deployedContracts.ts` with the Staker (standard SE-2 flow; the frontend's `\"Staker\"` reference resolves only after it exists there).\n- **Set real `NEXT_PUBLIC_ALCHEMY_API_KEY` / WalletConnect ID** via env for production RPC (the scaffold ships shared defaults).\n\nI did not run the app or deploy, per your instructions — the committed code is the deliverable.","ttft_ms":33406,"ttft_stream_ms":2759,"time_to_request_ms":64,"type":"result","duration_ms":118913,"uuid":"fd1021f9-6a2b-43e7-9118-d21fb2db2ced"}
